The readers are patched from their current definitions (one match each, marker `LEAVE1-C5`); flags and columns are unchanged (`take_access_ended`, `claim_ended`), so the app needs no change. A take or claim recorded after the restore counts (new row with the new sequence). Nothing is rewritten; `handle_exception`, the P2 → P1 clock and the QG1 rules are unchanged.
- **Claims without a recorded time (PO 14:30, (2): "Engineering says so and proposes").** Every claim made through `set_quality_event_status` has its transition row, so in the app there is none. A row set to `in_review` outside the command (as the table owner) has no time to compare with; it is read as **lapsed once the owner has any `ended` access event** (the safe side: the team sees "Übernahme beendet – Zugang beendet" and handles it, rather than reading a claim nobody may hold). Rows written before 0085 (null `access_seq`) are judged by time, as C3 does.
- **COND1-5b.** `private.working_time_notice_version()` returns `work1-notice-v3`; `NOTICE_VERSION` and `NOTICE_PARAGRAPHS` (src/features/work/rules.ts) carry v3: every v2 statement stays; added (i) "Dieses Protokoll liest die Administration deiner Organisation; es wird nur für einen angegebenen Prüfzweck gelesen." (ii) the next sentence begins "Ausserhalb einer solchen Prüfung sieht die Administration nur" (iii) the covering member (Vertretung) sees reason and author of a correction (iv) coordination roles outside the team see that a visit time was corrected (old and new value, time of the correction), not the reason and not who corrected (RLS3-4b view (b), 0070) (v) no export after her access ends; days not exported by then stay unexported (`begin_command` refuses an ended person, LEAVE1-3). A v1 or v2 acknowledgement does not carry over; `acknowledge_working_time_notice` refuses both (`invalid`). Still marked as the employer's draft.

**Deviations (for the PO):** (19) COND1-8: a quality claim with no recorded `in_review` transition is read as ended once its owner has any access end (proposal above); (20) COND1-8: the access sequence is also recorded on comments and closes (one trigger per table; only takes and `in_review` transitions are read); (21) COND1-5b (iv) also says "nicht, wer korrigiert hat", which is what RLS3-4b returns to those roles; (22) COND1-5b (i) is worded as who reads the log, without "only the administration": other readers of the audit log (owner operations, G-19) are not ruled out by this text.

**Not chosen:** rewriting `owner_id` or adding a `claimed_at` column to `quality_events` (the transition log already holds the time; nothing rewritten); allowing a re-claim of an `in_review` event whose claim lapsed (QG1 rules unchanged; the team can still close it).

**Rollback:** re-apply the three readers from 0080 (or patch `signal_take_lapsed(...)` / `quality_claim_lapsed(...)` back to `access_ended(...)`); drop the two triggers; the columns can stay. Version: a later migration sets the next version (the employer's text, G-19 item 2).

**Tests:** pgTAP 085 (25: take and claim before the end record 0; end → untaken/ended; restore → still untaken, flagged, no name in any Operations or team row; nothing rewritten; take and claim after the restore count; claim without a transition read as ended); 083 (27, +1: v1 and v2 acknowledgements do not carry over, v2 refused, v3 accepted); 079 and 081 acknowledge v3; vitest `work-rules` (v3 statements (i)-(v) and the fixed phrase); Playwright `work.spec` (the v3 phrase at 390 px). Run here: migration 0085 twice on a local PG16 with stub tables and the readers of 0080 (patch markers, predicates through end, restore and re-take); the text assertions under node. pgTAP, vitest, lint, typecheck and Playwright not run here; CI decides.

### ADR-099 — IPA1-1 to IPA1-6: no client risk wording, no client ranking in React, the `/matching` scorer removed

**Date:** 2026-10-26 · **Status:** IMPLEMENTED in the app (in PR; CI decides). IPA1-7 and IPA1-8 PLANNED in a second PR (remediation plan `docs/agents/clinical/remediation/CGR-69.md`) · **Brief:** `docs/agents/briefs/IPA1.md` (PO 2026-10-26, amended 16:20 and 18:10)

**Context:** The D-34 assessment (F-1, F-5) found legacy texts and client-side logic that contradict the intended-purpose statement: the pre-recovery `/operations` dashboard filtered and sorted client cases in React as "Top Risiko-Fälle" (age, missing team, `urgency = 'hoch'`), gave each team a "risk score", ran a client-side intake-age rule ("SLA Monitor", "SLA-Verletzungen", "24–48h gefährdet"); SIG2 texts called staffed hours "überwachte Zeiten"; the deprecated `/matching` demo kept a team-request scorer (ADR-006).

**Decision:**
- **IPA1-1 (remove, not rename).** The "Top Risiko-Fälle" panel, the "SLA Monitor" panel and the "SLA-Verletzungen" KPI are removed with their helpers (`getCaseAgeHours`, `getSlaStatus`). No list replaces them: the cases that need a decision are already in the OPS1 exception list, derived in the database with OPS1's thresholds and priorities (case not ready, offer overdue, candidates exhausted, no deadline). No new read model, so no second ordering rule. The case drill-down, reachable only from the removed panel, goes with it. The KPI "Qualitätsrisiken" becomes "Schwere Qualitätsereignisse" (still the count of OPS1 `quality_serious_open` exceptions).
- **IPA1-2.** The team risk score (`getTeamRiskScore`, `getRiskColor`) is removed. "Team Health" becomes "Pflegeteams", listed **by name** (de-CH collation) with utilisation, free slots and status; "Kapazitätsmonitor" becomes "Auslastung je Pflegeteam".
- **IPA1-3.** "überwachte(n) Zeit(en)" → "besetzte(n) Zeit(en)" in `src/features/signals/rules.ts`, `src/features/push/rules.ts`, `src/app/(protected)/admin/outside-section.tsx` (also the accessible labels "Besetzte Zeiten <Tag>"). Identifiers (`OUTSIDE_WATCHED_*`, `watched_hours`) and behaviour unchanged. No SQL returns this text (checked).
- **IPA1-4 (removed).** `src/app/(protected)/matching/` (page and `matching-demo.tsx` with `calculateMatchScore`) is deleted, with its sidebar entry. `/matching` now answers 404 (signed in) or redirects to `/login` (no session), as before behind the flag. A unit test fails if the route directory reappears or a source file carries the scorer or a `"/matching"` link.
- **IPA1-5.** `tests/unit/ipa1-wording.test.ts` scans every `.ts/.tsx/.js/.mjs` file under `src/` with comments stripped (line numbers kept) and fails on `Risiko|Überwachung|überwacht|Gesundheitszustand|Diagnose` (case-insensitive) outside an allow-list of `{file, phrase, reason}`. The allow-list is empty; a stale entry fails. "Rettungsdienst alarmiert" and "Sicherheitssignal" contain none of the words.
- **IPA1-6.** e2e at 390 px on `/operations`: the removed texts are absent, the OPS1 exception list and the seeded P1 serious event are unchanged.

**Brief question (other client-side rules in the legacy client), answered:** still in React, all on **team or organisation facts, not client cases**: colour thresholds of the KPI cards (average utilisation > 90 / ≥ 80, free slots < 5 / ≤ 10, cases without team > 5 / ≥ 3) and of the per-team utilisation bar (> 90 / ≥ 80). The counts "Aktive Klientenfälle" and "Offene Teamzuweisungen" filter cases by status and assigned team (organisational facts, no ranking, no risk label). Left as is; listed for the PO. The dashboard query still loads `care_visits`, now unused by the page: data minimisation follow-up (removing the read changes the query's type; not in IPA1's scope).

**Not chosen:** a server read model for "Fälle ohne Team" (duplicates OPS1's case-not-ready and candidates-exhausted exceptions with a second ordering rule); keeping the demo behind the flag with a bundle assertion (removal is simpler and IPA1-4 accepts it).

**Rollback:** revert the PR (no migration).

**Tests:** vitest `ipa1-wording` (new), `signals-rules`, `push-rules` (texts updated); Playwright `operations.spec` (IPA1-6, new), `push.spec` (labels updated), `auth.spec` (`/matching` 404 unchanged). Run here: the new unit test under node with a minimal vitest shim (green on the branch; on unchanged `recovery/main` src it reports the old hits in `operations-client.tsx`, `outside-section.tsx`, `push/rules.ts` and `signals/rules.ts`); syntax check of the changed files with the TypeScript transpiler. vitest, typecheck, lint and Playwright **not run here** (no npm registry access); CI decides.

### ADR-100 — IPA1-7 and IPA1-8: the intake urgency has no preset and a recorded source; the referrer's requested first-visit date

**Date:** 2026-10-26 · **Status:** IMPLEMENTED in migration 0086 and the app (in PR; CI decides) · **Brief:** `docs/agents/briefs/IPA1.md` (PO addendum 2026-10-26 18:10) · **Finding:** `cra` CGR-69 (advisory, S2; H-19, PS-58), remediation plan `docs/agents/clinical/remediation/CGR-69.md`

**Context:** The intake form preselected "mittel" and recorded no source; `team_offers` orders a team's offers by that level (G3). The record could not tell a referrer's statement from the intake person's reading (D-34 question 7). The referrer's requested first-visit date was not recorded (hypothesis P-10).

**Writers of an intake urgency (analysed):** `public.record_partner_request` (0010) is the only command that takes a level. `convert_partner_request` (0013) and `link_returning_referral` (0034, "return" path) copy `partner_requests.urgency` into a new case; `private.open_matching_if_ready` (0034) copies `cases.urgency` into the matching request. No command updates an urgency afterwards. The "resume" path of `link_returning_referral` keeps the paused case's level and source (unchanged behaviour).

**Decision:**
- **Columns.** `urgency_source text not null default 'unknown'` (check `referrer_stated | intake_assessed | unknown`) and `referrer_requested_start date` on `partner_requests`, `cases` and `matching_requests`. Existing rows read `unknown` ("Quelle nicht erfasst"); nothing is back-filled as a recorded value. The default stays `unknown`, so any writer that does not name a source records exactly that, never a guess.
- **Command.** `record_partner_request` gains `p_urgency_source` and `p_requested_start` (appended, default null); the old 11-argument signature is dropped. It refuses an empty level (`urgency_required`), a missing source or `unknown` (`urgency_source_required`) and a date before the request's creation day in Europe/Zurich (`requested_start_before_request`), all `22023`. A table check repeats the date rule on `partner_requests`.
- **Travel.** BEFORE INSERT triggers copy the source and the date from the request into the case (`private.case_takes_request_provenance`) and from the case into the matching request (`private.matching_takes_case_provenance`). This covers both case paths and the matching path without redefining the three large commands.
- **Team view.** `team_offers` returns `urgency_source` and `referrer_requested_start`; its body is 0034's with two columns added, kept in 0074's LEAVE1-3 form (plpgsql, the access guard first, `return query`), and the ORDER BY (G3) is byte-identical. `/team` shows "Dringlichkeit: <Stufe> (vom Zuweiser angegeben | bei der Aufnahme eingeschätzt | Quelle nicht erfasst)" and "Gewünschter Beginn (laut Zuweiser)". `/intake` shows the same line on each open request.
- **Form.** Level and source are required selects with an empty "bitte wählen" choice (no preset); the date field is optional with `min` = today (Zurich). zod refuses a missing level or source.
- **Audit.** The three audit triggers also record `urgency_source` and `referrer_requested_start` (class `code` in the AUD1-1 catalogue). The recording person is the audit actor; no new person field.
- **No rule.** NAVORA sets no criteria for a level or for choosing a source. Whether an intake person may choose "bei der Aufnahme eingeschätzt" at all, and on which criteria, stays with `clinical` (D-34 question 7). Until answered, both values are offered, as the brief says.

**Deviations (for the PO):**
1. **Two "Gewünschter Beginn" facts.** `cases.requested_start` (0013) already exists: the intake person records it in the case facts, it is readiness item 6 ("Gewünschter Beginn fehlt"), and `/team` already shows it as "Beginn". IPA1-8's date is the referrer's statement, so it is a separate column `referrer_requested_start` (not `requested_start` as the remediation plan said). It does not pre-fill or replace the intake fact and is not part of readiness. Proposal: keep both, labelled by source; the PO decides whether the intake fact should be pre-filled from the referrer's date.
2. The date rule is "not before the request's creation day" only, as the brief says; no upper bound is added (the intake fact keeps its own −30/+366-day range).
3. `cases` gets no new column grant (teams read the source through `team_offers` only). `partner_requests` and `matching_requests` keep their table-level SELECT for coordinators under RLS, so coordinators can read the new columns there.
4. The old 11-argument call is refused (no source), not kept as an overload, so no caller can record a level without its source.

**Not chosen:** redefining `convert_partner_request`, `link_returning_referral` and `open_matching_if_ready` (three large bodies to copy for two columns, more regression risk than a copy trigger); a back-fill of existing rows as `referrer_stated` (would invent a provenance).

**Rollback:** a forward migration that restores 0034's `team_offers` and 0010's `record_partner_request` signature and drops the two triggers; the columns can stay (no reader left).

**Tests:** pgTAP `086_ipa1_7_8_urgency_source` (29): refusals (no level, empty level, no source, `unknown`, old call form, date before creation), storage on request, case and matching request, audit with the recording person, `team_offers` returns source and date, G3 order unchanged with and without a date and for each source, no other function reads the date, the LEAVE1-3 guard is kept. pgTAP 012, 013, 014, 017, 018, 019 updated to pass a source. vitest `intake-rules` (source labels). Playwright `intake.spec` (new 390 px test: no preset, required, `/team` shows level and source and the referrer's date), `journey.spec` (chooses level and source). Run here: migrations 0001-0086 and the seed on a local PostgreSQL 16 (Supabase auth stubs), 0086 applied twice; the touched pgTAP files under a minimal local pgTAP stand-in (every file in `supabase/tests/database` run before and after this change: the only difference is the new file 086, 29/29; the stand-in's own failures, privilege and extension artefacts, are identical on both sides). The first CI run caught two regressions the first local run had not covered: `team_offers` had lost the LEAVE1-3 guard (pgTAP 074:217), and native `AT TIME ZONE` was used (pgTAP 068). Both are fixed and both files were rerun; TypeScript syntax check of the changed files. vitest, typecheck, lint, real pgTAP and Playwright **not run here** (no npm registry access, no pgTAP package); CI decides.

### ADR-101 — COND2: the unexported-days count only in the recorded access-end step; take-over of a lapsed quality claim; no visit list for `/operations`

**Date:** 2026-10-27 · **Status:** IMPLEMENTED in migration 0087 and the app (in PR; CI decides) · **Brief:** `docs/agents/briefs/COND2.md` (PO 2026-10-27 01:40) · **Conditions:** COND1-C1 (COND1 deviation 16), COND1-C2 (`cra` CGR-70, PS-59), IPA1-C1 (IPA1 deviation 3)

**Context:** Three accepted items left small pre-pilot conditions (G-13, G-19). (1) `member_unexported_working_days` (0083) answered an administrator of the person's organisation at any time; WORK1-C3 (b) "no standing per-person view of export habits" held by the UI only. (2) Since LEAVE1-C5 (0085) a lapsed `in_review` quality claim could only be closed, which invites closing an event unreviewed. (3) `/operations` still read every `care_visits` row of the organisation, with the nurse's name, and serialised it to the browser, although IPA1 (ADR-099) removed every panel that showed it.

**Decision:**
- **COND2-1, recorded prepare (the brief's open question).** The standing reader `member_unexported_working_days` is **dropped**: an administrator calling it outside the end step gets no count (the call fails, "function does not exist"). Opening the access-end step calls the new command `prepare_member_access_end(person, key)`. It runs through `begin_command` (idempotency, LEAVE1-3 guard), uses the scoping of `end_member_access` (`private.leave1_org_for`: an administrator of an organisation of hers, not herself, her access still open) and writes **one audit row per call** (`audit_events`, table `organization_memberships`, entity = the person, command `prepare_member_access_end`, changed `{"access_end_prepared": true}`; key classified `code` in the AUD1-1 catalogue). The audit row says who opened the step for whom and when; it **never holds the count** (WORK1-C3 (b)). The result carries `unexported_days` only on `ok`; `rejected`, `self`, `unchanged` and `duplicate` carry none. `end_member_access` keeps the count in its result (0083); `my_unexported_working_days` on `/work` is unchanged.
  - **Why not "only in the end result":** the administrator's prompt "Sie hat n bestätigte Tage noch nicht exportiert. Bitten Sie sie, diese vorher selbst zu exportieren" (G-19 item 4) must appear **before** the end; after the end she can no longer export. A recorded prepare keeps that prompt and turns a silent read into an accountable one. An administrator can still open the step for any member at any time; what changes is that each such read is recorded under her name. Whether that is enough for the employer's information duty stays with `lca` (G-19 item 2, D-23); the PO may withdraw COND2-1 per the brief.
  - **Not chosen:** a dry run of `end_member_access` (would need the full end body twice and still be callable at any time, unrecorded); keeping the old reader as a null stub (a dead API that looks like a reader).
- **COND2-2, take-over.** `set_quality_event_status(…, 'in_review')` is accepted from `in_review` when, and only when, `private.quality_claim_lapsed(event, current owner)` (0085) is true. The permission check is the existing `private.can_handle_quality_event` (open → in_review), no new role. The update sets the new owner (audited by the 0020 trigger on `owner_id`) and appends one `quality_event_log` row in_review → in_review with actor, key and `access_seq` (0085 trigger). Nothing is rewritten; the first claim's log row stays. A restore of the former owner gives nothing back (readers judge the current owner and the newest in_review row, which is the new owner's). A live claim stays `rejected`. `/field` shows "Übernehmen" on an event in review whose claim has lapsed (`canTakeOver`).
- **COND2-3.** `getOperationsDashboard` no longer reads `care_visits`; `OperationsVisit`, `OPERATIONS_VISIT_COLUMNS` and the `visits` key of `OperationsDashboard` are removed. RLS on `care_visits` is unchanged (coordination still reads it for `/visits`); `READ_PATHS.md` updated.

**Deviations (for the PO):**
1. COND2-1 "refused or null": **refused** (the function no longer exists), as permitted by the brief.
2. COND2-1: after her access has ended the step returns `unchanged` without a count; the administrator saw the count in the end result. pgTAP 083's last assertion changes accordingly (`null/1` → `null/null`) and its administrator reads go through the new step.
3. COND2-1: the read record is an `audit_events` row, not an `audit_read_log` row: `audit_read_log` (AUD1-5) records reads of the audit log for a purpose code (`compliance`, `billing`, `safety_review`); none of those codes fits, and adding a purpose code is not Engineering's call.
4. COND2-2: the former owner, once restored, may take over a claim that is still lapsed (e.g. her own earlier claim on another event), like any team member: a take recorded after the restore counts (LEAVE1-C3/C5 rule). She cannot take back a claim another member holds.
5. COND2-2: the rejection text for a live claim is the existing QG1 text ("bereits geschlossen oder nicht für dich sichtbar"); no new text was added.
6. COND2-2 e2e needs a lapsed claim in the seed: synthetic `austritt.test` (…907, org A, in no team, never signs in), seed event 704 on visit 502 (Testteam Nord, low severity) in review by her, then her access end (as the table owner). No other seed row changes.

**No rule:** no signal, exception, threshold, quality severity, hold rule or read scope is added or widened; what counts as "too long in review" stays D-16 (`clinical`).

**Rollback:** a forward migration that restores 0083's `member_unexported_working_days`, drops `prepare_member_access_end` and restores 0020's in_review condition; the app change reverts with the PR.

**Tests:** pgTAP `087_cond2_unexported_read_quality_takeover` (36): the old reader is gone; the step is for signed-in users only; the administrator gets the count; the read is recorded without the count; a repeated key records nothing; RN, Operations, coach and another organisation's administrator get none; no self-prepare; refused calls record nothing; `/work` count unchanged; COND2-2 (a)-(e) and the unchanged QG1 paths; no read widened. pgTAP 083 updated (deviation 2). vitest `cond2-operations-payload` (no `visits` key, no `care_visits` read, OPS1 calls unchanged) and `cond2-rules`. Playwright `quality.spec` (390 px: "Übernahme beendet – Zugang beendet", "Übernehmen", "in Prüfung" after reload); `work.spec` (end-step count) and `operations.spec` (IPA1-6) unchanged and expected to pass. Run here: migrations 0001-0087 and the seed on a local PostgreSQL 16 with Supabase auth stubs, 0087 applied twice; every pgTAP file under a minimal local pgTAP stand-in (all pass except three schema-only files the stand-in cannot run: 001, 007, 053); the seed block checked by a manual take-over as …902. vitest, typecheck, lint, real pgTAP and Playwright **not run here** (npm registry unreachable, no pgTAP package); CI decides.

### ADR-102 — COND2-4: a resumed case records the returning referral's urgency, source and requested start

**Date:** 2026-10-27 · **Status:** IMPLEMENTED in migration 0088 and the app (in PR; CI decides) · **Brief:** `docs/agents/briefs/COND2.md` (COND2-4, added by po 2026-10-27 02:10) · **Condition:** IPA1-C2 (`cra` CGR-71)

**Context:** `link_returning_referral` (0034) has two paths. "Return" (closed case) inserts a new case, so the 0086 BEFORE INSERT triggers copy the request's `urgency_source` and `referrer_requested_start`. "Resume" (paused case) inserts no case and no matching request: the new referral's level, source and date stayed on `partner_requests`, which the team cannot read, and the team kept reading the first referral's values. ADR-100 recorded this as unchanged behaviour; CGR-71 asks for the remedy.

**Decision (the brief's choice: record on the case):**
- **Resume path.** After the case facts, one update sets `cases.urgency := request.urgency`, `cases.urgency_source := request.urgency_source` (a request recorded before IPA1-7 carries `unknown`) and `cases.referrer_requested_start := coalesce(request date, case date)` (a request without a date never blanks the case's date). The three keys join the existing change_log diff of the resume (one row: old and new values, the linking person as actor, the link reason, the resume's idempotency key, command `update_case_intake`); `audit_cases` (0086) records the same in `audit_events`. Nothing earlier is rewritten: each return adds its own row.
- **Body.** `link_returning_referral` is the current body (0034 in the DST1 clock form, `private.now()` / `private.at_zurich`) with that one step and the extended diff list; the "return" branch is byte-identical. `convert_partner_request` is untouched.
- **Team view (where it handles the return).** `team_cases` returns two more columns, `return_urgency_source` and `return_referrer_requested_start`, **only while the case's `review_after_pause` task is open** (null otherwise). `team_cases` already returned `urgency`; its ORDER BY (name) and LEAVE1-3 guard are kept (patched by marker, as 0034 patched it). `/team` shows, inside the review task, "Dringlichkeit: <Stufe> (<Quelle>)" (the IPA1-7 label, "Quelle nicht erfasst" for `unknown`) and "Gewünschter Beginn (laut Zuweiser): <Datum | nicht angegeben>".
- **Display only.** A resumed case keeps its team and its existing matching request, so `open_matching_if_ready` does not run and `team_offers` (G3) is not reached; no order, filter, deadline, signal, readiness item or visit reads the new values (pgTAP 086's allowlist of readers of the date gains exactly `link_returning_referral` and `team_cases`).

**Deviations (for the PO):**
1. **Scope of the team read:** the source and the referrer's date are shown only while the return's review task is open, not permanently on the case card. This is the narrowest read that meets "where it handles the return" and avoids a new standing read of intake facts (ADR-100 (3): teams read case provenance only where they decide). After the task is closed the card shows no level line, as before (`urgency` was returned but never displayed on the case card).
2. **The level is recorded too**, not only the source and the date: a source without its level would be wrong ("hoch (vom Zuweiser angegeben)" under the old level). The brief names `urgency` among the three values.
3. A later re-matching of the same case (only after a release, which creates no new matching request today) would read the case's newest level; that is the "one source" the brief prefers and changes no order now.
4. The e2e test pauses and resumes seed case Testfall 08 inside `intake.spec` and closes the review task at the end, so later specs see the seed state (plus the higher level, which no other spec reads).

**No rule:** which level or date the care decision relies on stays `clinical` (D-15, P-14); NAVORA sets no criteria for a level or a source (D-34 question 7). No signal, exception, threshold, quality severity, hold rule or read scope is widened; no person gains a read of a case she could not read.

**Amendment 2026-10-27 07:40 — COND2-4a (po 06:10, `cra` CGR-73), before merge, in 0088:** the case still keeps its stored date when a new request has none (unchanged), but `team_cases.return_referrer_requested_start` is no longer `cases.referrer_requested_start`: it is read from **this return's request**, i.e. the case's newest `referral_links` row of kind `resume` (by `created_at`, then `id`) joined to its `partner_requests` row. A request without a date (also one recorded before IPA1-7) reads null, which `/team` shows as "nicht angegeben"; a kept date from an earlier referral is never shown as this referrer's statement. Why the newest resume link is this return: the review task is open only after the latest resume, and two resumes of one case are separate transactions with a pause between them. Edited in 0088 rather than a new 0089 because 0088 is not merged and was never applied outside CI and local runs (create-or-replace and marker patch unchanged in form). The app is unchanged: it already printed "nicht angegeben" for null. pgTAP 088 now 33: the second return (no date) reads no date in the team view while the case keeps 4 days and the first return's date stays in `change_log`; the pre-IPA1-7 return reads no date and the case date is unchanged. ~~In the test the earlier links are aged by three days as the table owner, because all steps run in one transaction (same `created_at`).~~ (Superseded in 10745eb: `referral_links` is append-only, so the test moves the pinned clock between returns instead.) Run here: nothing; the local pgTAP install was not available in this run. CI decides.

**Amendment 2026-10-27 10:20 — CGR-74 (`cra` 09:30, advisory S3, fix before merge), in 0088 step 1b:** the 10745eb note that `created_at` defaults to `private.now()` was wrong: 0034 set `default now()`, which is fixed for a whole transaction, so in pgTAP 088 all three resume links tied on `created_at` and the `id` tie-break picked one by a random uuid (088 passed by chance). 0088 now runs `alter table public.referral_links alter column created_at set default private.now()` (idempotent; ADR-081 clock, as the tables since 0073). Production is unchanged: there `private.now()` is `now()` and each resume is its own transaction. Chosen over a different order key because the newest link by time is the meaning, and the default is what the test clock is for. pgTAP 088 now 35: the three resume links carry three different `created_at` values, and the column default contains `private.now()`. Run here: nothing locally; CI decides. IMPLEMENTED (pending CI).

**Rollback:** a forward migration restoring the previous `link_returning_referral` body (the resume step removed) and `team_cases` without the two columns; recorded values stay (audited).

**Tests:** pgTAP `088_cond2_4_resume_takes_referral_urgency` (31): pause, link a returning referral with `hoch`, `referrer_stated` and a date → the case, change_log (old and new of all three, actor, reason, command) and audit_events hold them; a replay writes nothing; no new case, the matching request and the visits unchanged; the team's RN and care assistant read all three in `team_cases`, a nurse of another team and Operations read nothing; after the task is closed the return fields are null; a second return without a date keeps the date (no date key in change_log); a referral recorded before IPA1-7 reads `unknown`; every earlier level stays in the trail in order; `convert_partner_request` still carries the values; `team_offers` output and ORDER BY and `team_cases` order and guard unchanged. pgTAP 086 (allowlist of readers of the date). vitest `returning-rules` (`returnReferralFacts`). Playwright `intake.spec` (390 px: pause on /team, record and link on /intake, the review task shows "Dringlichkeit: hoch (vom Zuweiser angegeben)" and the date, no horizontal overflow, task closed). Run here: migrations 0001-0088 and the seed on a local PostgreSQL 16 with Supabase auth stubs, 0088 applied twice; every pgTAP file under a minimal local pgTAP stand-in, before (on `recovery/main` 7f21eb4) and after this change: the only difference is the new file 088 (31/31); the first run after caught that the copied 0034 body had lost the DST1 clock form (pgTAP 068 tests 42, 43), fixed and rerun; the stand-in's own failures (schema-only files it cannot run) are identical on both sides. TypeScript syntax check of the changed files and a node check of `returnReferralFacts`. vitest, typecheck, lint, real pgTAP and Playwright **not run here** (npm registry unreachable, no pgTAP package); CI decides.

### ADR-103 — UX1 parts (a), (b), (e): one page frame that collapses below 768 px, a menu that mirrors each page's own check, CI screenshots

**Date:** 2026-10-27 · **Status:** IMPLEMENTED in the app and CI (in PR; CI decides) · **Brief:** `docs/agents/briefs/UX1.md` (UX1-1 to UX1-5, UX1-14) · **Issues:** #506, #507, #512

**Context:** `/settings`, `/admin` and `/operations` used a fixed `280px 1fr` / `240px 1fr` grid (horizontal scroll at 390 px). `Sidebar.tsx` showed every item to every role, "Qualitätsmanagement" had no link, and `/teams`, `/visits` (first MVP) were menu items. `uxa` reviews UX1 from CI screenshots, which did not exist. The brief allows two PRs under one hand-off and asks for (e) first; this PR carries (a), (b) and (e). Parts (c), (d), (f) follow in a second PR.

**Decision:**
1. **Page frame (UX1-1).** `AppFrame` in `components/layout/Sidebar.tsx`: one column below 768 px, `md:grid-cols-[280px_1fr]` from 768 px (the `/intake` pattern), the content column `min-w-0` so content cannot widen the page. Below 768 px the side column is replaced by a bar on top: "Navora", "Abmelden" (always one tap) and a closed `<details>` "Menü" with the same items. Used by `/settings`, `/admin`, `/operations`. On `/operations` the two-column team section and the five KPI cards wrap (`lg:grid-cols-2`, `grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-5`), the team table scrolls inside its panel, and the header has a minimum instead of a fixed height. No wording changed (part (d)).
2. **Menu by role (UX1-3).** `src/lib/auth/nav.ts` is the one table route → page check. A page check is not always a role list, so the table names the database predicate each page uses: `/admin` = `private.is_org_admin` (admin); `/intake` (and the demo `/partners`) = `private.is_intake_role` (admin, operations); `/operations` = `private.is_coordinator` (admin, coach, operations: `operations_exceptions` returns nothing to anybody else); `/analytics` = coordinator **or** team member (`pilot_kpis`); `/field` = team member (`field_visits` reads the caller's own `team_memberships`); `/work` and `/settings` = every member (own rows only). The protected layout reads the caller's own `organization_memberships.role` and whether she has a `team_memberships` row (`getNavAccess`, as herself, filtered to her user id; fails closed to no item) and hands them to the menu through a React context. **The menu is not a boundary:** every page and command keeps its own check, unchanged. A unit test reads the newest definition of each predicate from `supabase/migrations` and fails when a role list drifts, and checks that `operations_exceptions`, `admin_members`, `pilot_kpis` and `field_visits` still use the predicate the table assumes.
3. **Items (UX1-4).** "Qualitätsmanagement" removed (no quality page exists; open holds and serious events are on `/operations`). `/teams` and `/visits` are no longer menu items; the routes and their pages are unchanged until UX2. Labels unchanged (part (d) with the `cra` string list).
4. **Screenshots (UX1-14).** CI job `ui-screenshots`: local Supabase with the synthetic seed, `next start`, `playwright.screenshots.config.ts` (`tests/screenshots/`), full-page images of `/login` and of every route each seed person (nurse, coach, operations, admin) may open, from the same table as the menu, plus `/team` for team members and `/teams`, `/visits` (every member, until UX2), at 390 × 844 and 1440 × 900. Uploaded as the artifact `ui-screenshots` (14 days), never committed (`/ui-screenshots/` git-ignored). It fails only on a render error (HTTP ≥ 500, an uncaught page error, the Next.js error page). It runs on every push to `recovery/main` and on PRs whose diff touches `src/app/` or `src/components/` (otherwise the job passes with a notice).

**Deviations (for the PO):**
1. **UX1-2:** the e2e cannot complete the opt-in in CI. The SIG2 e2e does not stub a provider: without a recorded DPF entry for a push vendor the channel stays off (fail closed, `push.spec`), and recording one is an `lca` decision, not a test fixture. `ux1.spec` therefore checks the SIG2 state a nurse sees at 390 px ("nicht aktiv", the member information and the channel-off reason, inside the page width, no sideways scroll). The switch itself at 390 px stays PLANNED until a test-only channel exists (pgTAP 064 tests the delivery rules inside a transaction).
2. **`/work` for every member:** the database admits every member to her own working day, so the menu shows it to coaches, Operations and administrators too. Whether WORK1 is meant for non-care staff is not decided here (UNKNOWN; `po`).
3. **No `/team` item:** the brief does not ask for one; `/team` stays reachable from `/field` ("Zum Team").
4. **`security-headers.spec`** (selector change only): its client-side navigation step clicked the menu item "Einsätze" (`/visits`), which is no longer a menu item; it now follows "Meine Einstellungen" and checks that page's heading.
5. Below 768 px the bar shows the existing product name "Navora" (part (d) decides the name).

**No rule:** no migration, RLS policy, grant, database function or page access check changes; no signal, threshold or text fixed by `cra` or `lca` changes.

**Rollback:** revert the PR (no data or schema involved).

**Tests:** vitest `nav-access` (predicate role lists read from the migrations; items per seed person; fail closed). Playwright `ux1.spec` (390 px: `/settings` as nurse, `/admin` and `/operations` as admin, `/operations` as Operations and coach, `scrollWidth <= 390`; the collapsed menu; per role the exact items, each opened without a refusal; a nurse typing `/admin` or `/intake` still gets the existing refusal). `ui-screenshots` job. Run here: TypeScript transpile check of the changed files and a node check of the predicate parser and the items (the npm registry and the Supabase images were not reachable from this run). vitest, lint, typecheck, build and Playwright **not run here**; CI decides.

### ADR-104 — UX1 part (c), UX1-20, UX1-21: next visit first, one filled button per card, "Mehr" for cancel and hold, a header menu on the nurse's screens, a scannable WORK1 notice

**Date:** 2026-10-27 · **Status:** IMPLEMENTED in the app (in PR; CI decides) · **Brief:** `docs/agents/briefs/UX1.md` (UX1-2 amended, UX1-6 to UX1-10, UX1-20, UX1-21) · **Issues:** #509, #548, #553

**Context:** `/field` showed the next visit about 1,100 px down (signals, workday link, counters, absence, "Offen im Team" first), every action was a filled button, and "Einsatz absagen" and "Abrechnungssperre setzen" sat at the weight of "Losfahren". A nurse could not reach `/settings` (SIG2 push opt-in) from `/field`, `/team` or `/work` (`uxa` UXR-10, PO 16:25: rides part (c)). The WORK1 notice was six unheaded paragraphs (`uxa` UXR-3; `lca` 11:41 gave a lead line and six headings). Part (d) (texts, `cra` string gate) and part (f) (sign-in, contrast) follow in a third PR.

**Decision:**
1. **Order (UX1-6).** `/field` main: (1) `SignalBanner`, unchanged in content, rule and position; (2) the next visit, labelled "Als Nächstes" (today) or "Erster offener Einsatz" (another day); (3) her other visits of the day, in the order `field_visits` returns them; then the open decisions and reports (CGR-25 "Entscheid offen", F6R-5 "Zurückgegeben", F7 "Zur Gegenzeichnung"), unchanged; (4) "Offen im Team", collapsed, its count in the header; network covers (COVER1), team open reports (LEAVE1-12), the team's absences (ABS1); (5) the workday link and the three counters. One `<ol aria-label="Einsätze">` keeps every visit card (next one first). The next visit is chosen by `pickNextVisit` (`features/field/rules.ts`, unit-tested): planned or running, not suspended; hers (responsible: du) before the team's; a running one before the earliest start; none → the list stays in time order. It is display only and assigns nothing.
2. **Open items stay in sight.** Because the CGR-25, F6R-5 and F7 lists now sit below her visits, a one-line "Zu erledigen" row under the signals links to each of them with its count, shown only when one is non-empty. Contents, rules and test ids of the lists are unchanged.
3. **Absence and "Offen im Team" (UX1-6a).** "Ich kann nicht arbeiten" is a compact outline control in the `/field` header row (first viewport, two taps: open, save); the form opens over the page (`role="dialog"`), so the page order is unchanged. Its fields, defaults, the ABS1-C6 start limit and the CGR-32 confirmation with "Zum Team" are unchanged; after a save with past visits the dialog shows that confirmation until "Schliessen". The team's absences (withdraw, shorten) moved into their own section below the visits. "Offen im Team" is collapsed ("Alle anzeigen"); while collapsed its header shows the earliest visit of today she may take with "Übernehmen" (`earliestTakeableToday`, unit-tested), so taking stays one tap.
4. **One filled button per card (UX1-7).** At rest a card has at most one filled button: its status action ("Losfahren", "Einsatz starten", "Einsatz abschliessen", "Fortsetzen"), or the report action of a completed visit, or "Ergebnis erfassen" after a no-show, or the follow-up decision of an interrupted visit. The always-open "Neue Beobachtung" form's "Beobachtung speichern" became an outline button. Forms opened by a tap keep their filled submit (it is that form's one action).
5. **"Mehr" (UX1-8, UX1-9).** "Einsatz absagen" (planned or on the way) and "Abrechnungssperre setzen" (no open hold) are in a "Mehr" menu at the bottom of the card: tap 1 opens it, tap 2 opens the existing form, which first states the consequence in one sentence (`CANCEL_CONSEQUENCE`, `HOLD_CONSEQUENCE`); the hold keeps `HOLD_MEANING` (CGR-13) unchanged. The commands (`cancel_visit`, `set_quality_hold`), their reasons, audit records and refusals are unchanged. "Ereignis melden" stays one tap, an outline button on the card (PO decision). "Nicht angetroffen" and "Nicht abgeschlossen beenden" stay on the card as outline buttons (no billing or planning consequence named in the brief).
6. **Header menu (UX1-20, UXR-11).** `HeaderMenu` (`components/layout/Sidebar.tsx`): one "Menü" control (≥ 44 px) in the header of `/field`, `/team` and `/work`, inside the first viewport at 390 × 844, opening the same items as the side column (the UX1-3 table, not an access boundary) with "Abmelden" as the last item. The `/field` header's separate "Abmelden" text button moved into it. In the phone bar of `/settings`, `/admin`, `/operations` (#535) "Abmelden" moved from the bar into "Menü" as its last item; the bar's summary reads "Navora · Menü" (name: part (d)). The header rows wrap instead of widening the page. UX2-1 later replaces this control with the tab bar.
7. **WORK1 notice (UX1-21).** `NOTICE_LEAD` and `NOTICE_HEADINGS` (`features/work/rules.ts`), word for word from `lca` 11:41, rendered as a lead paragraph and one `h3` per paragraph. The six paragraphs, the draft hint and "Gelesen und verstanden" are unchanged; nothing is collapsed. `NOTICE_VERSION` stays `work1-notice-v3` (PO decision).

**Deviations (for the PO and `cra`):**
1. **Cancellation consequence wording.** The brief's example ("the visit is released to the team") is not what `cancel_visit` does: it stores the reason and sets the visit `cancelled` (migrations 0024, 0054); nothing is offered to the team. The sentence states what happens: "Der Einsatz wird mit dem gewählten Grund als abgesagt gespeichert und findet nicht statt." Both consequence sentences wait for `cra` review before merge (UX1-12 gate; the brief asks `cra` to review the hold wording).
2. **Position of the open decisions and reports.** The brief's order names (1) to (5) only. CGR-25, F6R-5 and F7 lists are placed after her visits with the "Zu erledigen" link row at the top (decision 2). F6R-5 said "stays at the top of her day"; `cra` reviews the order (UX1-6 clinical gate) and may move CGR-25 above the next visit.
3. **UX1-6 measure.** The e2e uses the seed nurse `pflege.test` on the day after today (seed visit 508, 09:00, planned) at 390 × 844. SIG1 signals depend on the clock in the seed; when one is shown, the assertion allows exactly the signal banner's height (the signal stays above everything, as the brief requires) and checks the DOM order.
4. **E2E selector changes (UX1-10):** `field.spec` ABS1 opens "Offen im Team" ("Alle anzeigen") before taking the three-days-ahead visit; `field.spec` CGR-34 and `quality.spec` QG1 tap "Mehr" before "Einsatz absagen" / "Abrechnungssperre setzen", and `quality.spec` checks the item's absence on the card instead of inside the quality section; `ux1.spec` (#535) now expects "Abmelden" inside "Menü" (UXR-11) and starts the UX1-2 opt-in check at `/field`; `intake.spec` (IPA1-7/-8 at 390 px) opens "Menü" before "Abmelden". The UX1-8 e2e plans its own visit (six days ahead, 05:00) because seed visit 508 is no longer planned when `ux1.spec` runs. No assertion about a rule, text or state changed.
5. UX1-2 opt-in still ends at the "nicht aktiv" state with the channel-off reason (ADR-103 deviation 1, unchanged).

**No rule:** no migration, RLS policy, grant, database function, page access check, signal rule, threshold, priority, recipient or text fixed by `cra` or `lca` changes; the WORK1 lead line and headings are `lca`'s wording.

**Rollback:** revert the PR (no data or schema involved).

**Tests:** vitest `ux1-field-order` (next-visit choice, order, the "Offen im Team" header row, consequence sentences, `HOLD_MEANING` unchanged, WORK1 lead and headings word for word, version unchanged). Playwright `ux1.spec` (390 × 844: UX1-2 from `/field`; UX1-6 next visit in the first viewport and below the signals, open team visits, workday link and counters after it, absence control in the viewport; UX1-7 filled buttons per card on two days; UX1-8/UX1-9 two taps with the consequence, nothing saved, "Ereignis melden" one tap; UX1-20 from `/field`, `/team`, `/work` to `/settings` in two taps, control in the viewport and ≥ 44 px, "Abmelden" last; UX1-21 lead line and headings equal the constants, lead and first heading in the first viewport). Run here: TypeScript syntax check of every changed file (the npm registry was not reachable from this run: ENOTFOUND). vitest, lint, typecheck, build and Playwright **not run here**; CI decides.

**Addendum 2026-10-27 19:15 (`cra` 17:35, UX1-20 note):** "Meine Einstellungen" is the nurse's only route to the SIG2 push opt-in, so it no longer depends on the membership read. `menuItemsFor` (`lib/auth/nav.ts`, pure) is `navItemsFor` plus the settings item when the role table left it out (a failed read returns `NO_ACCESS`; outside the provider likewise). Every other item stays fail-closed; `navItemsFor` and the table-vs-migration test are unchanged. No access widens: `/settings` reads only the caller's own rows (`public.my_push_state`). Tests: vitest `nav-access` (failed read → only `/settings`; equal to `navItemsFor` whenever settings is already admitted). Not run here (npm registry unreachable); CI decides.

**Addendum 2026-10-27 20:30 (`uxa` 19:51 UXR-13, UXR-14, UXR-16; PO 20:15 on #554: UX1-25 is a merge condition, UX1-26 rides the same push):**
1. **UX1-25.** The absence form is a native modal `<dialog>` (`showModal()`): focus moves to the first field ("Wer"), the page behind is inert, Tab wraps at both ends inside the dialog, Escape closes it without sending (the dialog's `cancel`), and every close (Escape, "Abbrechen", "Schliessen", a save without past visits) returns focus to "Ich kann nicht arbeiten". Fields, defaults, the ABS1-C6 limit and the CGR-32 confirmation are unchanged.
2. **UX1-26.** `HeaderMenu` closes on Escape (focus back on "Menü"), on a pointer press outside it and when a link inside is chosen. Items and order unchanged.
3. **UXR-16.** "Abrechnungssperre setzen" in "Mehr" is a neutral outline (stone), no longer rose (P1 colour family); the consequence sentence is unchanged.
Tests: Playwright `ux1.spec` UX1-25 (focus inside after the tap, 30 Tabs stay inside, Escape closes, focus back on the trigger, nothing recorded) and UX1-26 (Escape with focus back on "Menü"; outside tap). Not run here (npm registry unreachable); CI decides. No rule, text, command or access check changed.

### ADR-105 — UX1 part (f): one sign-in page for every role, readable secondary text, inputs inside their form, light colour scheme

**Date:** 2026-10-27 · **Status:** IMPLEMENTED in the app (in PR, stacked on #554; CI decides) for UX1-15, UX1-16, UX1-17, UX1-19; **PLANNED** for UX1-18 · **Brief:** `docs/agents/briefs/UX1.md` (UX1-15 to UX1-19) · **Issues:** #515, #516, #553

**Context:** `/login` used a fixed two-column inline-styled grid (`1.05fr 0.95fr`) and addressed only "Admin / Coach" (UXR-1, severe); 58 text elements used a colour opacity of 50–70 % (UXR-2, high; the brief counted 57); `/intake` inputs in `grid-cols-2` tracks overflowed their form at 390 px because `1fr` tracks keep the input's intrinsic minimum (UXR-6); `globals.css` flipped `--background`/`--foreground` under `prefers-color-scheme: dark` while every page hard-codes light surfaces (UXR-8).

**Decision:**
1. `/login`: Tailwind layout, one column below 768 px with the sign-in card first and a short "Geschützter Zugriff" panel below it; from 768 px the panel sits left. One heading "Bei NAVORA anmelden". The role-specific panel ("Admin / Coach Portal", "Attention Center", "Operations-Lage", "Teams Übersicht", "Navora Admins und Coaches") is removed; the security sentence and the card's paragraph are kept word for word. Inputs 16 px (no iOS zoom). `signIn`, the session check, the redirect, the error text and the proxy are unchanged.
2. One class `text-secondary` (`@utility` in `globals.css`, `stone-700`, the interim value of `DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` §7; UX2-3 turns it into the token) replaces every `text-<colour>-<n>/50|60|70`. stone-700 on white is about 10:1 and stays above 4.5:1 on the light emerald, amber, rose and stone tints used on these pages. Text at `/80` is allowed by UX1-17 and stays. No wording change.
3. `/intake`: `field` and `sel` input classes get `w-full min-w-0`; the form grids use `minmax(0, …)` tracks, so an input can never be wider than its track.
4. `:root { color-scheme: light }` and the dark override removed until a dark theme is designed; the screenshot job adds one `/field` capture with `colorScheme: 'dark'`.
5. **UX1-18 (axe `color-contrast` in the screenshot job, failing on serious/critical) is PLANNED for the next PR.** Reason: it needs a new dev dependency (`@axe-core/playwright`, lockfile change) and its first run will report contrast violations outside the 58 replaced uses (e.g. `/80` tints, badges, first-MVP inline-styled pages) that must be fixed or listed as exceptions with reasons; the npm registry was not reachable from this session, so the dependency and its lockfile could not be generated or checked locally.

**Deviation:** the brief says the explanatory panel goes "below it or removed"; it is kept below, reduced to the one security sentence that applies to every role.

**No rule:** no migration, RLS policy, grant, database function, page access check, signal rule or `cra`/`lca`-fixed text changes.

**Rollback:** revert the PR (no data or schema involved).

**Tests:** vitest `ux1-contrast` (UX1-17 scan of `src/app/(protected)`, `src/app/(auth)`, `src/components`; the class exists; UX1-19 (ii) `color-scheme: light`, no dark override; UX1-16 string scan of `src/app/(auth)/login/*` without comments; one heading). Playwright `ux1.spec` part (f): UX1-15 at 390 × 844 (no sideways scroll, e-mail, password, "Anmelden" in the first viewport, inputs ≥ 300 px), UX1-19 (i) at 390 and 1440 px (every visible input, select and textarea inside its form). `auth.spec` selector change: heading "Admin / Coach anmelden" → "Bei NAVORA anmelden". Screenshot job: `nurse_field_dark_390x844`. Not run locally (no npm registry in this session); CI is the test runner.

### ADR-106 — UX1-18 and UX1-27: axe `color-contrast` in the screenshot job; open work stays in sight above the next visit

**Date:** 2026-10-28 · **Status:** IMPLEMENTED in PR (CI decides) · **Brief:** `docs/agents/briefs/UX1.md` (UX1-18 as sharpened by po 20:15 for `uxa` UXR-15; UX1-27 for `cra` CGR-80, po 22:15) · **Issue:** #569

**Context:** ADR-105 item 5 left UX1-18 PLANNED: the screenshot job (UX1-14) never checked contrast, and `/teams`, `/visits`, `/partners`, `/operations` still use inline greys `#6b8075` (4.2:1 on white, 3.9:1 on `#f4f7f4`), `#5f766d`, `#6d857c` (4.0:1 / 3.7:1) and `#6B7280` (4.5:1 / 4.5:1), and 16 elements use `text-stone-500` (about 4.8:1 on white, below 4.5:1 on `stone-100`). The sign-in button's pending state used white on `#6b8075` (4.2:1). UX1-6 (ADR-104) moved the open interruption follow-ups and returned reports below her visits; the "Zu erledigen" row at the top is the control that keeps them in sight, but no test held it there (CGR-80), and its link read "Entscheid offen (n)", without the words that say why the decision is open.

**Decision:**
1. **axe in the screenshot job.** `tests/screenshots/capture.spec.ts` runs axe-core's `color-contrast` rule on the whole document of every page it captures (every role, 390 and 1440 px, `/login`, the dark `/field` capture). A violation of impact serious or critical fails that person's test after all her routes are captured, with route, selector and axe's summary in the failure. The findings are written to `ui-screenshots/axe_<role>_<viewport>.json` for `uxa`. **No exception is listed.**
2. **How axe gets in.** The npm registry is not reachable from the engineering session, so the lockfile cannot be regenerated or checked here. The CI step `Fetch axe-core (exact version)` runs `npm pack axe-core@4.10.2` (npm checks the tarball against the registry's integrity hash), unpacks it under `/tmp/axe` and sets `AXE_CORE_PATH`. The test injects `axe.min.js` with `page.evaluate` (DevTools protocol, not a `<script>` element), so the app's CSP stays as it is; `@axe-core/playwright` is not used. TECH_DEBT TD-30: move axe-core to a devDependency with a regenerated `package-lock.json` once the registry is reachable.
3. **Colours.** `src/lib/ui/colors.ts` exports `SECONDARY_TEXT = "var(--color-stone-700, #44403b)"`, the value of the `text-secondary` class (ADR-105 item 2); every inline grey listed above becomes `SECONDARY_TEXT`; every `text-stone-500` becomes `text-secondary`. The pending sign-in button becomes `#37554a` (white on it is above 7:1). Colour only; no text, layout or behaviour change. vitest `ux1-contrast` fails on any of the four greys or `text-stone-500` under `src/app/(protected)`, `src/app/(auth)`, `src/components`.
4. **UX1-27.** `src/features/field/todo.ts` holds `FOLLOWUP_HEADING = "Nicht abgeschlossen: Entscheid offen"`; the "Zu erledigen" link, the section's `aria-label` and its `h2` all use it, so the link reads "Nicht abgeschlossen: Entscheid offen (n)" like the section heading. Order unchanged: SIG1 signals, then the "Zu erledigen" row, then the next visit (UX1-6). No section, rule or count changes.

**Deviations:**
1. `/partners` is a demo page behind `NEXT_PUBLIC_FEATURE_DEMO_PAGES` (ADR-006), off in the CI build, so it answers 404 and no role can open it there; the screenshot job does not capture it. Its inline colours are fixed (item 3) and held by the vitest scan, not by axe. A capture with the flag on needs a second build; not done.
2. The UX1-27 e2e allows the first-viewport bound to grow by the height of a SIG1 banner, as UX1-6 does: the seed's signals are clock-dependent and SIG1 always comes first.
3. The first CI run is the first contrast measurement of these pages. A serious or critical finding outside the replaced colours is fixed in this PR, never listed as an exception for `/teams`, `/visits`, `/partners` (brief).

**No rule:** no migration, RLS policy, grant, database function, page access check, signal rule or `cra`/`lca`-fixed text changes. The link wording is the section's existing heading (po 22:15, CGR-80 (b)).

**Rollback:** revert the PR (no data or schema involved).

**Tests:** vitest `ux1-contrast` (UX1-18 scan; `SECONDARY_TEXT` value), `ux1-todo` (UX1-27 (b): the constant's wording; link, `aria-label` and heading use it; no shorter link text). Playwright `ux1.spec` "UX1-27 at 390 × 844": a seed nurse ends a visit on day +8 at 20:00 as not completed, then on the day with seed visit 508 the `todo-links` row is rendered, inside 390 × 844 (plus a SIG1 banner's height), above `next-visit` by DOM order and `boundingBox().y`, after the SIG1 signals, and the link text equals the section heading; the follow-up is then decided through the product. Screenshot job: axe `color-contrast` on every capture. Not run locally (no npm registry in this session); CI is the test runner.

### ADR-107 — UX1 part (d), first PR: the absence dialog while it sends, its heading, "Zu erledigen" link = section heading, `/login` panel removed

**Date:** 2026-10-28 · **Status:** IMPLEMENTED in PR (CI decides) · **Brief:** `docs/agents/briefs/UX1.md` (UX1-28 with (d), (e) of po 02:10; UX1-29; UX1-30 with `cra` 05:30 conditions (1)-(3), po 06:10; UX1-16 as widened by po 02:10 for `uxa` UXR-17) · **Issue:** #585

**Context:** `uxa` UXR-18: "Abbrechen" and Escape could close the "Ich kann nicht arbeiten" dialog while the save was still running, so a nurse could miss the refusal or the CGR-32 notice that her absence covers planned visits before today. UXR-19: the dialog had no visible heading and was named "Abwesenheit", a second label for the control "Ich kann nicht arbeiten". UXR-20: the "Zu erledigen" links "Zurückgegeben (n)" and "Zur Gegenzeichnung (n)" were literals separate from their section headings, and the returned-reports heading read "Zurückgegeben: bitte korrigieren und neu unterschreiben (n)", so the link and the heading named one thing twice. UXR-17: `/login` carried a panel "Geschützter Zugriff / Alle Seiten erfordern eine Anmeldung. Die Sitzung wird serverseitig geprüft." that no user can act on.

**Decision:**
1. **UX1-28.** While `recordAbsence` is pending: "Abbrechen" is `disabled`; the dialog's `cancel` event is prevented (`onCancel`); and a window keydown listener (capture phase, only while pending) cancels Escape, so the close request never starts. The second guard covers browsers that skip a repeated `cancel` event without user activation (close-watcher rule). The outcome is shown in the still-open dialog: `command-error` on a refusal; on success with `past_planned > 0` the existing `absence-past-planned` notice with "Zum Team", kept until she closes the dialog; on success with 0 the dialog closes as before. No text, command, field or ABS1 rule changes.
2. **UX1-29.** A visible `h2` "Ich kann nicht arbeiten" is the first element inside the dialog and its accessible name (`aria-labelledby` with `useId`); the dialog's `aria-label="Abwesenheit"` is gone. The form keeps its name "Abwesenheit erfassen" (selectors of the ABS1 e2e unchanged).
3. **UX1-30.** `src/features/field/todo.ts` holds one constant per section (`FOLLOWUP_HEADING`, `RETURNED_HEADING = "Zurückgegeben"`, `COUNTERSIGN_HEADING = "Zur Gegenzeichnung"`) and one label function per section (heading + " (n)"); the link, the section's `aria-label` and its `h2` use them. The returned-reports heading becomes "Zurückgegeben (n)"; the instruction moves word for word into `RETURNED_INSTRUCTION = "bitte korrigieren und neu unterschreiben"`, rendered in sentence case only ("Bitte korrigieren und neu unterschreiben") as the section's first line, unconditionally, directly after the `h2` and before the list (`cra` (1), (2)). The returner's name, the reason on each item and the visit-card labels in `features/field/report.ts` are unchanged (`cra` (3)). The fallback (link = full old heading) is not needed.
4. **UXR-17.** The `/login` panel is removed and nothing replaces it; the page is one column with the sign-in card at every width. The UX1-16 string test also fails on "serverseitig". Sign-in logic, session check, proxy, error texts and the development banner are unchanged.

**String list (UX1-12, for `cra`):**
| Where | Old | New |
|---|---|---|
| `/field` returned-reports `h2` | "Zurückgegeben: bitte korrigieren und neu unterschreiben (n)" | "Zurückgegeben (n)" |
| `/field` returned-reports, first line (new position, same words) | — | "Bitte korrigieren und neu unterschreiben" |
| `/field` returned-reports `aria-label` | "Zurückgegeben" | "Zurückgegeben" (from the constant) |
| `/field` absence dialog accessible name | "Abwesenheit" | "Ich kann nicht arbeiten" (visible `h2`) |
| `/login` side panel | "Geschützter Zugriff" / "Alle Seiten erfordern eine Anmeldung. Die Sitzung wird serverseitig geprüft." | removed, no replacement |

**Deviations:**
1. UX1-28 (c) and (d) need a planned visit before today in the seed. The one such state is created by `field.spec.ts` "ABS1-C6" (assist.test absent from yesterday, visit 532); the UX1-28 (c), (d) assertions are added to that test (no existing assertion changed) instead of a second test, which would find the state already consumed by CGR-32/CGR-34. (a), (b), (e) and UX1-29 are in `ux1.spec.ts` "UX1 part (d)".
2. UX1-28 (b) provokes a refusal by sending a member id that belongs to no team (the database answers `rejected`, nothing is recorded); the UI offers no refusable choice that leaves no data behind.
3. UX1-28 (e), second half, records a one-day absence for the synthetic assist.test 200 days ahead (no visit of hers is planned then). It is synthetic seed data in the CI database only.
4. Not run locally: the npm registry is not reachable from the engineering session (as in ADR-106), so typecheck, lint, vitest and Playwright run in CI only.

**CGR-85 (`cra` 09:30, advisory S3, fixed in this PR):** UX1-28 holds Escape and "Abbrechen" while the save is pending, so a save that throws (lost connection) would have left the dialog stuck on "Wird gespeichert …" with no outcome. `useAbsenceRun().run` now resets `pending` in `finally` and turns a throw into `ABSENCE_MESSAGES.failed` as `command-error` inside the open dialog, then `router.refresh()`. A retry is safe (an overlapping absence is refused as `overlaps`, 0046). Playwright `ux1.spec` "CGR-85": the save aborted by `page.route` (`internetdisconnected`), dialog open, `command-error` = `failed`, no past-visit notice, "Abbrechen" enabled, Escape closes it, nothing recorded. UX1-28 (a)-(e) unchanged.

**Still PLANNED in part (d):** UX1-11 (string scan for internal codes), UX1-12 for those strings, UX1-13 (one product name, `/operations` "Ausnahmen", `nav.ts` labels = page headings). Part (g) UX1-22 to UX1-24 PLANNED.

**No rule:** no migration, RLS policy, grant, database function, page access check, signal rule or `cra`/`lca`-fixed text changes.

**Rollback:** revert the PR (no data or schema involved).

**Tests:** vitest `ux1-todo` (all three links = section `h2` via one constant each; `cra` (1) position and unconditional rendering, (2) constant equals the existing wording, sentence case only, (3) returner/reason line and `report.ts` labels unchanged), `ux1-contrast` (UX1-16 "serverseitig"; panel gone). Playwright `ux1.spec` "UX1 part (d) at 390 × 844": UX1-29 (named by the visible `h2`, first element, no `aria-label`); UX1-28 (a) Escape and disabled "Abbrechen" with the save held by `page.route`, (b) refusal inside the open dialog, (e) no past-visit notice on refusal, and a save without a past visit closes the dialog. `field.spec` "ABS1-C6" (assertions added): (a) while held, (c) notice and "Zum Team" inside the open dialog, still there after the refresh, (d) text equals `pastPlannedNotice(1)`; "F6R" returned-report test (assertions added): link text = `h2` "Zurückgegeben (n)", instruction line visible.

### ADR-108 — UX1 part (d), second PR: no internal codes in page text, one product name, one label per page

**Date:** 2026-10-28 · **Status:** IMPLEMENTED in PR (CI decides; `cra` string gate UX1-12 before merge) · **Brief:** `docs/agents/briefs/UX1.md` (UX1-11, UX1-12, UX1-13 as widened by po 2026-10-27 16:25 for `uxa` UXR-12) · **Issue:** #592

**Context:** page text showed decision codes ("(D-3)", "Entscheid D-26 offen", "Bedingung SIG1-8", "(D-22)", "(D-15)", "D-11") that no nurse can act on, three product names ("Navora", "Swiss Care OS", "Navora MVP") and English labels ("Operations Board", "Control Tower", "KPI / Analytics", "Drilldown"). Pages had several names: menu "Mein Tag (mobil)", header "NAVORA · Mein Tag"; link "Teamanfragen" to a page headed "Dein Team"; link "Teamzahlen" to "KPI / Analytics".

**Decision:**
1. **Scan from the syntax tree (UX1-11).** `tests/unit/support/user-facing-strings.ts` parses each `.ts`/`.tsx` file with the TypeScript compiler API (already a devDependency) and returns its string literals, template literal parts and JSX text. Comments are not literals, so they are exempt without a regex. Skipped as not user-facing: module specifiers, directives, and the values of attributes that never reach the reader (`className`, `style`, `data-testid`, `href`, `id`, `key`, `type`, `name`, `htmlFor`, `role`, form attributes, `aria-labelledby`/`-describedby`/`-controls`). `aria-label`, `title`, `placeholder` and `alt` are scanned. Scope: `src/app/(protected)`, `src/app/(auth)`, `src/components` (with `components/layout`) and `src/lib/auth/nav.ts`.
2. **Reviewer reference = `data-ref`.** A code that reviewers need goes into the `data-ref` attribute of the element whose text it qualifies. It is in the DOM and the screenshots' HTML, not on screen. No tooltip, because a visible tooltip with a code would be user-facing again. The scan exempts the `data-ref` value only. An element marked `data-ref` keeps its visible text scanned.
3. **One product name (UX1-13).** The side column reads "NAVORA". "Swiss Care OS" and the "Pilot Schweiz / Navora MVP" box are removed. The phone bar reads "NAVORA". `/operations` is headed "Ausnahmen". The OPS1 panel heading "Drilldown" becomes "Pflegeteam Detail", the label that panel already shows once a team is selected.
4. **One label per page.** `PAGE_LABEL` in `src/lib/auth/nav.ts` is the only source. It holds the eight menu pages plus `/team`, which is not in the menu but is linked from `/field`. The menu item, the page's name and every header link to it from another page use the constant. The page's name is its `h1`/`h2`, or on the day pages (`/field`, `/work`) the line above the date, because there the `h1` is the date. An eyebrow line that was a second name for the page is removed: "Operations", "Aufnahme", "Einstellungen", "Administration", "Pilot-Wirkung", "NAVORA · Team". Its own day and month navigation ("Heute", "← Vortag", "Folgemonat →") is not a link from another page. Two inline action links are named exceptions, because they name an action, not the page: "Zum Team" (the CGR-32 notice confirmed by `cra`, kept word for word) and "Arbeitstag ansehen und bestätigen" (WORK1).

**String list (UX1-12, for `cra`):**
| Where | Old | New |
|---|---|---|
| `/field` "Offen im Team" note (cover) | "… wenn der Zugriff für Übernahmen geklärt ist (D-3); bis dahin …" | "… wenn der Zugriff für Übernahmen geklärt ist; bis dahin …" (`data-ref="D-3"`) |
| `/field` taken-over visit note (cover) | "… bis du ihn in NAVORA durchführen kannst (D-3). Das Team stimmt sich mit dir ab." | "… bis du ihn in NAVORA durchführen kannst. Das Team stimmt sich mit dir ab." (`data-ref="D-3"`) |
| `/admin` invitations | "… richtet vorerst die NAVORA-Betriebsverantwortung ein (Entscheid D-26 offen)." | "… (Entscheid offen)." (`data-ref="D-26"`) |
| `/admin` outside-app delivery | "Bis zur Freigabe durch die Clinical Owner bleibt die Bedingung SIG1-8 bestehen: jemand beobachtet die Signale in jeder geplanten Stunde." | "… bleibt diese Bedingung bestehen: jemand beobachtet die Signale in jeder geplanten Stunde." (`data-ref="SIG1-8"`) |
| `/team` information form | "… Frist und Inhalt der Information sind offen (D-22)." | "… sind offen." (`data-ref="D-22"`) |
| `/team` KLV category form | "… die fachliche Freigabe der Zuordnung ist offen (D-15)." | "… ist offen." (`data-ref="D-15"`) |
| billing preview | "nicht hinterlegt (Pilotkanton offen, D-11)" | "nicht hinterlegt (Pilotkanton offen)" (`data-ref="D-11"`) |
| side column | "Swiss Care OS" / "Navora" / "Pilot Schweiz" / "Navora MVP" | "NAVORA"; the rest removed |
| phone bar | "Navora" | "NAVORA" |
| menu | "Operations Board" / "Mein Tag (mobil)" / "KPI / Analytics" | "Ausnahmen" / "Mein Tag" / "Kennzahlen" |
| `/operations` | eyebrow "Operations", `h1` "Navora Operations Control Tower" | `h1` "Ausnahmen" |
| `/operations` team panel | "Drilldown" | "Pflegeteam Detail" |
| `/intake` | eyebrow "Aufnahme", `h1` "Zuweisungen aufnehmen" | `h1` "Aufnahme" (the description line is unchanged) |
| `/field` header | "NAVORA · Mein Tag"; link "Teamanfragen" | "Mein Tag"; link "Dein Team" |
| `/work` header | "NAVORA · Mein Arbeitstag" | "Mein Arbeitstag" |
| `/team` header | eyebrow "NAVORA · Team"; link "Teamzahlen" | removed; link "Kennzahlen" |
| `/analytics` | eyebrow "Pilot-Wirkung", `h2` "Kennzahlen {Monat}" | `h2` "Kennzahlen {Monat}" |
| `/settings` | eyebrow "Einstellungen", `h2` "Meine Einstellungen" | `h2` "Meine Einstellungen" |
| `/admin` | eyebrow "Administration", `h2` "Pilotbetrieb verwalten" | `h2` "Administration" (the description line is unchanged) |

The `cra`/`lca`-fixed substance (SIG1-8 condition, billing-estimate limits, IPA1 wordings, WORK1 notice, hold meaning) is unchanged. Only the code moved. The SIG1-8 note shown below it on `/admin` (`OUTSIDE_WATCHED_NOTE`) still carries its code; see deviation 1.

**Deviations:**
1. **Scope of the scan.** UX1-11 names TSX under `src/app/(protected)` and `src/components`. Message constants under `src/features/**` that pages render still carry codes: 33 strings in 9 modules (`analytics/hypotheses.ts` 14, `analytics/rules.ts` 8, `lifecycle/rules.ts` 3, `push/rules.ts` 3, one each in `billing/rules.ts`, `cover/rules.ts`, `quality/rules.ts`, `quality/commands.ts`, `signals/rules.ts`). Several are `cra`/`lca`-fixed (SIG1-8 note, billing limits "D-11, D-18", lifecycle D-19) and are pinned by unit tests word for word. PLANNED: a follow-up PR in part (d) widens the scan to `src/features/**` with its own string list for `cra`. It is not in this PR so that this list stays reviewable.
2. `/operations` keeps the exception center's own `h2` "Ausnahmen" below the page's `h1` "Ausnahmen". The page is that center, so one label names one thing. `auth.spec` asserts the `h1` by level.
3. Not run locally: vitest (`ux1-strings`, 8 tests) was exercised against a minimal local stand-in for the vitest API with the global TypeScript, because the npm registry is not reachable from the engineering session. Typecheck, lint, the real vitest and Playwright run in CI only.

**No rule:** no migration, RLS policy, grant, database function, page access check, signal rule or `cra`/`lca`-fixed substance changes. The menu's role table is unchanged; only labels moved into `PAGE_LABEL`.

**Rollback:** revert the PR (no data or schema involved).

**Tests:** vitest `ux1-strings`: UX1-11 no code in user-facing strings, a guard against an empty scan, and the moved codes present as `data-ref`; UX1-13 no forbidden product or English word and no old second name; menu labels = `PAGE_LABEL`, all different; each page names itself with `{PAGE_LABEL.<key>}`; every link to a labelled page from another page uses its label, except the two named action links. Playwright selectors updated: `auth.spec` (`h1` "Ausnahmen"), `ux1.spec` UX1-3 menu lists, `journey.spec` (link "Dein Team").

**Still PLANNED:** the UX1-11 widening to `src/features/**` (deviation 1); part (g) UX1-22 to UX1-24.

**Addendum 2026-10-28 13:45 (UX1-32, `uxa` UXR-22, UXR-23; rides #598 as the brief allows):**
- (a) `/partners` loses its eyebrow "Partner" above the `h1`. The `ux1-strings` scan gains an eyebrow check: a text-only element (literal text, no link, button, input or component inside) placed directly before an `h1` in the same parent, on any `.tsx` under `src/app/(protected)`. The header rows of `/field`, `/team`, `/work` carry links and the menu, so they are shell, not eyebrows; the day pages' label line is in that header row, not next to the date `h1`. A synthetic self-test proves the detector catches the removed `/partners` line.
- (b) The `/operations` team panel heading reads "Details zum Pflegeteam" (`uxa` wording) from `TEAM_PANEL_HEADING` in `src/features/operations/labels.ts`, in both the empty and the selected state; "Pflegeteam Detail" appears nowhere in user-facing text.
- **Deviation:** `/visits` (protected, not in the menu) had the same eyebrow "Einsätze" above its `h1`; the check "no protected page" fails on it, so it is removed too. No other text changes.
- **No rule:** no data, filter, rule, link, migration, RLS or access check changes. Not run locally beyond the vitest stand-in (11 of 11 in `ux1-strings`, and the new check fails on the old `/partners` file); CI decides.

### ADR-109 — UX1 part (g): one pointer line for an entry that repeats a signal card, red only for safety signals, team push reachability in sight

**Date:** 2026-10-28 · **Status:** IMPLEMENTED in PR (CI decides) · **Brief:** `docs/agents/briefs/UX1.md` (UX1-22 on `cra` C-1, UX1-23 on `cra` C-2 (a) and as widened by po 2026-10-27 20:15 for `uxa` UXR-16, UX1-24 on `cra` C-3) · **Issue:** #592

**Context:** `uxa` UXR-4: on `/team` an overdue or stuck visit appears twice, once as the SIG1 card at the top and once in full in "Einsätze mit Handlungsbedarf", and the long clearance paragraph pushes "Anfragen an dein Team" (the team's own decisions) down. UXR-5 (a), UXR-16: the referral urgency "hoch" on `/intake` and `/team`, two visit-status badges and the error lines on `/field` use the red family the design system reserves for P1 signals. UXR-7: the team's push reachability ("0 von 2 aktiv") sits in a closed, neutral `details`, so a team in which nobody receives P1 signals outside the app does not see it.

**Clinical gate (README, at claim):** UX1 is in scope. Latest report section read (2026-10-28 09:30, PR #589, not yet merged, and the merged 01:30 section); the findings that touch part (g) are C-1, C-2 (a), C-3 (`cra` 2026-10-27 13:37, merged with #532), placed word for word as UX1-22 to UX1-24. No CGR, Hazard Log or Safety Risk Register entry names `/team` presentation beyond these. No remediation plan needed (no finding creates a safety, billing or regulatory risk here; each one lowers one).

**Decision:**
1. **UX1-22 (C-1).** `alertRepeatsSignal(alert, openSignals)` in `features/team/rules.ts` decides, fail-safe, whether an entry shrinks to one line `<label> · <client> · Steht oben als Sicherheitssignal`. All must hold: an **open** card (the banner's open list; a taken card is compact, so it does not count) is for the same visit **and** the same situation (`signal_kind` = `exception_type`, i.e. `visit_overdue` or `visit_stuck`); the entry has no flags (CGR-32 "Zuständige Person abwesend gemeldet" keeps it in full); Operations has not taken it (`taken` is a handling state the card lacks); and it carries no action of its own (`visit_interrupted` with its follow-up form is always in full). Any other entry is unchanged. The card set is the one on screen: `SignalBanner` reports its polled rows through a new optional `onRows`, so a pointer never points at a card that has gone.
2. **UX1-22 clearance text.** The former paragraph is split, word for word, before its last sentence: `CLEARANCE_TEXT` behind a `details` "Wann erledigt sich das?" (`min-h-11` summary), `PROVISIONAL_NOTE` "Schwellen und Regeln provisorisch (klinische Entscheidungen offen)." always visible below it. A unit test asserts the two joined equal the former paragraph.
3. **UX1-23 (C-2 (a), UXR-16).** One map `URGENCY_BADGE_CLASS` in `features/intake/rules.ts` serves `/intake` and `/team`: "hoch" amber with a ring, "mittel" light amber, "niedrig" neutral; the word is kept. In `field-client.tsx` the status badges "cancelled" and "no_show" become neutral, and the three `command-error` lines use the design system's `error-inline` colours (amber-800 border, amber-50, stone-900 text) with their text unchanged. The two "Mehr" items were already outline and neutral (ADR-104); `field-client.tsx` now has no `rose-` class.
4. **UX1-24 (C-3).** `allReachable(rows)` in `features/push/rules.ts`. While not every listed (active) member has delivery outside the app on, the team status `details` is rendered `open`, amber (amber-800 border, amber-50), with `REACH_GAP_TEXT` "Nicht alle erhalten Sicherheitssignale ausserhalb der App" and a link to `SETTINGS_ITEM` ("Meine Einstellungen", `/settings`, from `nav.ts`, so it follows the UX1-13 label); collapsed and neutral when all are on. An empty list is not "all reachable". The per-member list is unchanged (active / not active, SIG2-2).

**Deviations:**
1. **UX1-23 test scope.** The brief's unit test "the `signal-p1` colour classes appear only in signal components" is implemented as: `border-rose-700` (the card's border, the class only the signal card uses) appears only under `src/components/signals`; the urgency map has no `rose-`; `field-client.tsx` has no `rose-`. Three filled `bg-rose-800` buttons outside the signal components remain and are OPEN for `uxa` (and `cra` for the first): `/field` "Ergebnis erfassen" on an overdue visit (`visit-lifecycle.tsx`, safety-adjacent, so not changed by engineering), `/team` "Beenden" (care-plan item) and "Entfernen" (planned visit; UXR-5 (b) waits for PLAN2). Error lines outside `/field` and the CSR1 list's red frame also keep their colours (design-system tokens are UX2's).
2. **UX1-22 pointer line content.** cra C-1 allows the pointer "only if the list entry adds nothing the card lacks". The card has no client name, so the pointer line keeps the alert's label and the client's name; it drops the alert's detail line, its age and the default handling sentence "Operations sieht es auch, noch nicht übernommen". Whether that default sentence is a "handling state" in C-1's sense is for `cra` to confirm on this PR; if it is, `alertRepeatsSignal` returns `false` for every entry and the list stays as before (one-line change).
3. **UX1-22 Playwright.** No seed state gives the nurse an open SIG1 card and its CSR1 alert at once without the signal evaluator's clock, so the pointer path is covered by the unit test; the e2e (in `lifecycle.spec` "CGR-25", the one test that creates a `visit_interrupted` alert) asserts the entry stays in full, the follow-up form opens in one tap, the clearance text is collapsed and opens on a tap, and the "provisorisch" sentence is visible without expanding. "Anfragen an dein Team moves up" follows from the shorter list and is not asserted as a position.
4. **Not run locally:** the npm registry is not reachable from the engineering session (as in ADR-106 to ADR-108), so typecheck, lint, vitest and Playwright run in CI only.

**Not changed:** no migration, RLS policy, grant, database function, signal rule, threshold, page access check or `cra`/`lca`-fixed text (the clearance paragraph keeps every word; C-3's wording is used as given). SIG1 cards stay first, unchanged in content, rule and position.

**Rollback:** revert the PR (no data or schema involved).

**Tests:** vitest `ux1-team` (`alertRepeatsSignal`: pointer only for same visit and situation, open card, no flags, not taken, not `visit_interrupted`; clearance text = former paragraph; C-3 wording; `allReachable`), `ux1-colour` (urgency map, shared use on `/intake` and `/team`, no `rose-` in `field-client.tsx`, `border-rose-700` only in signal components). Playwright `ux1-team.spec` "UX1-24" (seed team 0 of 2: open, `data-reach=gap`, text and link to `/settings` visible without expanding, amber, no overflow at 390 px); `lifecycle.spec` "CGR-25" (assertions added as in deviation 3).

**Addendum 2026-10-28 13:55 (UX1-33, `uxa` UXR-25; part (g), rides #602):** focus follows the absence dialog's result. When the CGR-32 notice replaces the form after a successful save, focus moves to "Schliessen" (effect on the notice state). After a refused or failed save (UX1-28 (b), CGR-85), focus returns to "Speichern", which was disabled while sending: an effect watches `pending` go from true to false and focuses "Speichern" when an error was set in the same render (`useAbsenceRun` sets both together). A save without a notice closes the dialog as before and focus returns to "Ich kann nicht arbeiten" (UX1-25). **No text, no rule:** the notice text (`pastPlannedNotice(n)`), its position, the link "Zum Team", the command and the UX1-28 and UX1-31 behaviour are unchanged. **Tests (CI decides; nothing run locally):** Playwright `field.spec` "ABS1-C6" (a) "Schliessen" focused after the notice; `ux1.spec` "UX1-28 (a), (b), (e)" and "CGR-85" (b) "Speichern" focused after a refused and a failed save, `command-error` in the open dialog.

### ADR-110 — COORD6: `agents-check` keeps frozen decision packages frozen (freeze guard)
