So the rule of three is applied **per cell** (agent, field, team, Zurich month): a cell with fewer than three distinct confirmers shows only the proposal count, all outcome counts null, `suppressed = true`. This also covers a team with fewer than three confirmers in the month (AN1). No org total is returned, so no cell can be recovered by subtraction. Readers: the team for its own rows, coordinators (`admin`, `coach`, `operations`, as OPS1) for all teams of the organisation.
9. **Stop rule (AIG1-8):** correction share = (corrected + rejected) / (confirmed + corrected + rejected) of the proposals created in the window, each by its newest confirmation; `not_found` and undecided proposals are not "decided". The minimum is a number of decided proposals. Windows are consecutive, `stop_window_days` long, from the switch-on; `private.aig1_check` runs on every proposal, bulk confirm and health read, evaluates the closed windows oldest first, and stops at the first whose share is **above** the threshold with at least the minimum. A changed version, provider or region against the switch-on also stops it. A stop writes `ai_agent_stops` (reason, window or period, counts, and the newest ledger and confirmation sequence numbers at that moment), sets `stopped`, and is audited as the system (actor null, `private.aig1_audit_system`). Restarting is a new switch-on (new window origin).
10. **OPS1:** exception type `agent_stopped` ("Assistent gestoppt", P2, closable as other P2 types; detail = the agent's name and the number of open look-back lines, "<Name> · <n> offen"; entity `ai_agent_stop`), derived while the agent stays stopped, so it closes with its cause on the restart. No new signal class (P2 sends no push). The brief's wording "Assistent gestoppt: <Name>" is shown as label plus detail.
11. **Look-back (AIG1-12):** `agent_stop_lookback(stop)`: records whose value from that agent was confirmed unchanged (newest confirmation `confirmed`, made before the stop) and proposed in the window (threshold) or since the switch-on (version change). Output: entity, field, team, case reference, visit date, proposal time; no confirmer, no outcome, no person filter. Coordinators see every line; a team member only lines of her teams' records. A re-confirmation or correction after the stop removes the line. **AIG1-13:** (a) a value that is already decided, every look-back value included, is never confirmed in bulk (the whole list is refused, 42501); (b) a listed value the record no longer holds (a later assignment or confirmation replaced it) stays on the list with `superseded = true` and `superseded_at` (the last audit entry that gave the record another value; null if unknown), and is not counted in the OPS1 detail "<Name> · <n> offen". The current value is read per field by `private.aig1_current_value` (only fields with a consuming command). No rule on whether or by when values are re-checked, superseded ones included (Clinical Owner, D-17; UNKNOWN).
12. **Evaluation harness (AIG1-9, open question 2):** each agent names a fixture function `private.aig1_eval_<key>()` (synthetic inputs, expected and actual values) and a bar; the test agent's rule is a pure function (`private.aig1_rule_primary_nurse`) used by both the command and the fixture. pgTAP 097 runs every agent's fixture and fails below its bar, so CI runs it on every PR. An agent without a fixture cannot be switched on.
13. **AN1 reuse (open question 3):** the rule (three) is reused; the mechanism is not (AN1 suppresses whole teams in its own readers), see 8.

**Trade-offs:** the generic `jsonb` value needs a per-field applier (`aig1_apply`): a new consuming field without one is refused (`0A000`) rather than written. The stop is evaluated lazily (no hosted job, D-2): a crossed window stops the agent at the next proposal, bulk confirm or health read, which is also the earliest moment it could do harm. Late confirmations of a window that already closed count at the next check. Because `aig1_check` writes, `agent_health` is VOLATILE.

**Deviations / OPEN:**
1. Part (b) not yet built: the confirm screen at 390 px (one value, "vorgeschlagen von", confirm, change, "nicht gefunden"), the ticked list (unticked, no select-all, count on the button, three of five), the health list at desktop width, the e2e link from the OPS1 exception to the look-back list (PLANNED, next PR).
2. The organisation's one-by-one list has no command (Clinical Owner role, G-1, OPEN); pgTAP fills it as the table owner.
3. Bulk requires the agent to be `on` (5): not in the brief; the PO may say otherwise.
4. The correction-share formula and "minimum = decided proposals" (9) are engineering readings of AIG1-8; the PO may say otherwise.
5. pgTAP 019 and 061 count 21 OPS1 rules; 077's writer list gains `aig1_audit_system` and its reader list `private.aig1_superseded_at` (reads the care_visits audit rows of one record for AIG1-13 (b)); 071 gains two catalogue assertions (AIG1-11 (4)).

**Not done (not coded):** no AI service, no provider, no real data (D-35 stage 0); no threshold value; no clinical rule; no re-check deadline; the live project is not touched (D-1).

**Checked by me (not CI):** migrations 0001-0097 and the seed on local PostgreSQL 16 with Supabase auth stubs and a minimal pgTAP stand-in; 0097 applies twice; every pgTAP file gives the same result as before 0097 (all green except 001, 007, 053, three schema-only files the stand-in cannot run), plus 097 110/110; with the ledger guard dropped and a select grant on `ai_confirmations`, 097 goes red. vitest, lint, typecheck, build, db lint, real pgTAP and Playwright **not run** (no npm registry here); CI decides.

**Rollback:** revert the commit; drop the seven `ai_*` tables and the AIG1 functions; delete the `agent_stopped` row from `exception_rules`; restore `private.derived_exceptions` from 0092. No existing record is changed by 0097 itself.

### ADR-121 — VIS2: a same-client overlapping visit is a fact before saving; same-slot cards are told apart (`cra` CGR-97)

**Date:** 2026-10-30 · **Status:** IMPLEMENTED in its own PR (migration 0098, pgTAP 098 and 050/052/093 (result key, signature strings), vitest `vis2-overlap`, Playwright `vis2.spec.ts`; CI decides) · **Brief:** `docs/agents/briefs/VIS2.md` (VIS2-1 to VIS2-5; VIS2-5 and the `uxa` wording added by po 2026-10-30 00:15) · **Issue:** #699

**Context (IMPLEMENTED before 0098):** `plan_visit`, `reschedule_visit` and `move_visit_to_today` check each visit on its own; none looks at the client's day (`cra` CGR-97). Two visits of one client at one time render as two `/field` cards with the same accessible name (CGR-96). VIS1-8 (0090) already states one yes/no fact about the date a move leaves, with the team boundary.

**Decision:**
1. **One overlap definition (open question 2):** `private.client_overlap_visits(case, date, start, end, exclude)`: every other visit of the case on that date, any team, not `cancelled`, whose planned wall-clock interval (`private.visit_wall_range`, FUP1-1: without an end, start + planned minutes A+B+C, else to the end of the date) meets the half-open interval `[date + start, date + end)`. Touching ends do not overlap. PLAN3 is not built yet; its member-overlap test should take the same half-open reading.
2. **One fact, one read path (open question 1):** `private.client_overlap_fact(...)` returns `{overlap, time_start, time_end}`; the times only of the earliest overlapping visit the caller can read (`private.reads_visit`); another team's visit makes `overlap` true without any team, member, service, time or count (VIS2-2). `public.client_visit_overlap(case, visit, date, start, end)` (SECURITY DEFINER, stable) serves the J3 planning form (case given), the /team move and VIS1-3b "Heute machen" (visit given; it is excluded). Visit given: answers only a team member of that visit (`private.visit_for_team`, as VIS1-8); case given: only a member of the case's assigned team (`private.reads_case`, RLS2). Anyone else, a missing user or an incomplete slot: null. No table grant changes.
3. **The sentence (VIS2-1, VIS2-5 (1)):** `uxa` wording (PO note 00:15): "Bei dieser Person ist <heute | am <Wochentag>, <Datum>> von <hh:mm> bis <hh:mm> bereits ein Einsatz geplant." / without a readable visit: "… zu einer Zeit, die sich mit diesem Einsatz überschneidet, bereits ein Einsatz geplant." `info`, below "Von"/"Bis", a polite `role="status"`, referenced by the save button's `aria-describedby`; re-read 250 ms after "Datum", "Von" or "Bis" change and when the form is offered again; an answer for an earlier slot is never shown. No overlap or no answer: no sentence (never "kein Einsatz"). Never blocks, delays or adds a field.
4. **Audit and the server re-check (VIS2-4, VIS2-5 (2)):** the three commands take `p_overlap_shown boolean default null` (what the form showed at the save) and record it as `overlap_shown` (code, AUD1 catalogue for `care_visits` and `visit_plan_changes`) on their existing audit row: `plan_visit`'s `care_visits` insert row (a `planned` plan change writes no audit row, trigger WHEN `change = 'moved'`), `reschedule_visit`'s `moved` row (also for `move_visit_to_today`, which passes it through). A transaction-local marker is set around that one insert and reset after it; `private.write_audit_event` reads it only for a `care_visits` insert, `private.audit_visit_plan_move` for its row. An `ok` result carries `overlap` = definition 2 at the save, whatever the form showed. When the server found an overlap the form did not show, the app states the sentence after the save: on the /team card, and for "Heute machen" in the moved card's notice on today's view (display-only parameter `ueberschneidung`, dropped from the address with the other move parameters, VIS1-3g).
5. **Signatures:** the old signatures are dropped and recreated with the extra defaulted argument (two overloads with defaults would make old calls ambiguous); old positional calls resolve unchanged and record nothing. Grants (`authenticated`, `service_role`) and comments re-applied. pgTAP 093 names the new signatures; 050 and 052, which compare a whole `ok` result, also strip the new `overlap` key (their assertions otherwise unchanged).
6. **Distinguishable cards (VIS2-3):** on `/field`, cards of the same case and day whose intervals overlap (same half-open reading; connected overlaps form one group; ordered by start, then id) carry "<i> von <n> zu dieser Zeit" in their accessible name (`uxa` wording) and one visible `info` line "<i> von <n> zu dieser Zeit · Verantwortlich: <du | Teammitglied | noch niemand>". Display only; one card per visit row.

**Trade-offs:** `overlap_shown` is a value from the browser (VIS2-5: it records what the form showed); it can be wrong if the browser is manipulated, which changes only that audit value, never the save. The server's own check is in the result and can be recomputed from the visits. Two generic trigger functions gain a guarded read of a transaction-local setting.

**Deviations / OPEN:**
1. VIS2-3 asks for the responsible member's display name in the card line; `field_visits` carries no responsible name (only `responsible_me`/`responsible_set`), so the line says "du", "Teammitglied" or "noch niemand", as the card's "Verantwortlich" row already does. Adding the name means a new column on `field_visits` (RLS4 keeps assignment columns team-readable); PLANNED if the PO or `uxa` wants it.
2. A visit of the previous date running past midnight is not counted (the brief: "on the target date"). OPEN whether it should be.
3. PLAN2 generated visits (`plan_from_care_plan`, regeneration) are not checked; the brief names planning, reschedule and "Heute machen". PLAN3's proposal step should call definition 1.
4. Whether a same-client overlap is allowed, needs a reason or is billable: Clinical Owner and billing owner (`cra` CGR-97, D-18), UNKNOWN; no rule is set.
5. Order: the watcher activated VIS2 (#699) at 00:07; the PO's 00:15 cycle lists CGR-96 (#693) and AIG1 part (b) as *Active now* and VIS2 as *Next up*. VIS2 was claimed at 00:13 and built; its e2e spec chooses today's slots where Testfall 08 has no visit at its start, so it adds nothing to the CGR-96 collision class. The PO decides whether it merges before CGR-96.

**Checked by me (not CI):** migrations 0001-0098 and the seed on local PostgreSQL 16 with Supabase auth stubs and a pgTAP stand-in; 0098 applies twice; pgTAP 098 40/40 and every other file green; the display rules ran in Node with a vitest stand-in. vitest, lint, typecheck, build, real pgTAP and Playwright **not run** (no npm registry here); CI decides.

**Rollback:** revert the commit; drop `public.client_visit_overlap`, `private.client_overlap_fact`, `private.client_overlap_visits`; drop the three 7/11-argument commands and restore them from 0050/0056/0093; restore `private.audit_visit_plan_move` from 0056 and `private.write_audit_event` from 0001; delete the two `overlap_shown` catalogue rows. No stored visit is changed by 0098.

### ADR-122 — RLS4: who did an act is read per record, never selectable or listed per person; who is responsible stays visible to the team

**Date:** 2026-10-30 (amended before merge the same night for RLS4-5, RLS4-6 and the RLS4-3 addendum, po 03:10) · **Status:** IMPLEMENTED in its own PR #715 (CI decides) · **Brief:** `docs/agents/briefs/RLS4.md` (po 2026-10-29 23:15; amended 2026-10-30 03:10) · **Findings:** `cra` 01:45 (PR #711): no clinical objection; conditions (a) SIG1 "übernommen von <Name>" stays per signal for every recipient, (b) report author and countersigner stay per report · `lca` 02:45 (D-23, advisory) read the keep-list on #715 · `aia` AUT-24 · **Migration:** 0099 · **Tests:** pgTAP `099_rls4_acts_per_record` (63); 071 one catalogue line (91); 016, 020, 022, 043, 049, 057, 096 read closed columns as the table owner or check a kept column; vitest `rls4-visits-responsible`; Playwright `access.spec.ts` (F0 by service, RLS4-6 names).

**Context (IMPLEMENTED before 0099, read in a database built from 0001-0098):** `authenticated` held a table-wide select on 15 of the 19 care-record tables below and column grants on four (`visit_reports`, `case_gp_notices`, `cases`, `safety_signals`) that included the act columns. A member of the record's team could select who decided, wrote, changed, completed, set, lifted, returned, countersigned, withdrew, created, recorded, confirmed, took, performed or handed back a cover, row by row through the Data API, and filter or count it per person. `authenticated` holds only select on these tables (every write is a SECURITY DEFINER command). No view and no RLS policy of another table reads these columns; `visit_report_returns`' own policy reads its `performer_id`, and a policy on the table itself needs no column grant (checked). The only INVOKER functions naming an act column are the trigger guards `guard_visit_report`, `guard_quality_hold`, `guard_safety_signal` (NEW/OLD comparisons, no select). The app reads four of these tables directly (`quality_holds` id, visit_id, reason, set_at; `case_gp_notices` case_id, informed_on, channel, recorded_at; `cases` CASE_SUMMARY_COLUMNS; `care_visits` on `/visits`, which read the legacy free-text `team_member_name`); e2e specs read none of the columns.

**Decision:**
1. **RLS4-1 and RLS4-5, mechanism (column grants, the RLS3-4 pattern of 0070 and 0096):** for each table `authenticated` loses the table-wide select and the closed columns, and keeps a column grant on exactly the other columns it could select before. The list is read from `has_column_privilege` in the migration, so a column closed before (the `cases` health columns, `visit_reports.snapshot`, …) stays closed: nothing is widened. The migration stops if a listed column does not exist. `anon` keeps nothing. 25 act columns on 19 tables: the brief's 22, plus (RLS4-5) `cover_events.nurse_id`, `visit_reports.performer_id`, `visit_report_returns.performer_id`.
2. **RLS4-6:** `care_visits.team_member_name` is not selectable either (not dropped: D-1 live reconciliation first). `/visits` reads `responsible_user_id` and the display name from `member_profiles` (readable within the organisation): "Pflegekraft: <Name>", "Noch offen" without a responsible member, "Teammitglied" when the responsible member has no display name. The app no longer reads or writes `team_member_name` (it never wrote it).
3. **Readers (RLS4-3):** every reader of the closed columns is SECURITY DEFINER and keeps its output; `READ_PATHS.md` names them per column. Per record to a member: `field_reports` (countersigner, returner, `i_am_performer`), `report_returns`, `field_returned_reports` (her own returned reports, the F6R work list), `field_countersign_queue` (performer per report to the team's registered nurses), `visit_correction_history`, `field_quality` ("gemeldet von", hold setter), `field_visit_status_notes`, `field_since_last` (HAND1 member codes; also a cover hand-back as a `moved` plan change with its reason), `my_signals` ("übernommen von <Name>" from `signal_takes`, addendum). No app query or INVOKER reader moves except `/visits` (decision 2).
4. **`quality_events.author_id`:** stays readable per record as "gemeldet von" through `field_quality` and `field_since_last`. No new reader.
5. **`cover_events.nurse_id` (RLS4-5):** read only by `accept_cover` and `withdraw_cover`. **Corrected 2026-10-30 05:45 (RLS4-5 (c), po 05:10):** `pilot_kpis` reads `cover_events` for `visit_id`, `kind` and `created_at` (cover share and time to acceptance per team), never `nurse_id`; its one `nurse_id` term is `handover_shown.nurse_id = performer` inside a `count(*)` share, and its result has no person column (pgTAP 099, two assertions). The earlier wording named it as a reader of the column because the 099 catalogue query matches `cover_events` and `nurse_id` anywhere in a function's source. Who covers now is `care_visits.covered_by` (kept). No reader returns who accepted or handed back a cover from `cover_events`; a hand-back shows per visit in HAND1 as the `moved` plan change with its reason and the member code (pgTAP 099 fixes the reader list).
6. **RLS4-2 (kept):** `cases.primary_nurse_id`, `deputy_nurse_id`, `care_visits.responsible_user_id`, `covered_by`, `quality_events.owner_id`, each listed with its task in `READ_PATHS.md`. 071 fails when a member can select any other person column on these 19 tables.

**Trade-offs:** a direct Data API read of a closed column fails with 42501; nothing in the app or e2e does it any more. A column added to one of these tables later is not selectable until its migration grants it (as `visit_events` since 0070). `select *` on these tables is refused; the app names its columns. `/visits` makes a second small query for display names.

**Deviations / notes for the PO:**
1. **`working_time_facts.performer_id`:** the brief says "her own rows stay readable to her through her own reader, WORK1". No WORK1 reader reads `working_time_facts` (IMPLEMENTED: `my_working_day` derives her day from visit events; only `private.build_billable_fact` reads this table, as owner), and no screen reads it. After 0099 nobody reads `performer_id` through the API, her own rows included. A reader of her own rows would be new (OPEN, PO).
2. **Act columns without a per-record reader:** `visit_reports.created_by`, `withdrawn_by`, `care_visits.suspended_by`, `case_tasks.done_by`, `cases.intake_consent_confirmed_by`, `team_decisions.actor_id`, `quality_event_log.actor_id`, `quality_holds.lifted_by`, `billable_facts.created_by`, `safety_signals.taken_by` (served from `signal_takes`), `referral_links.actor_id`, `working_time_facts.performer_id`, `cover_events.nurse_id` are returned to a member by no reader; they are read inside commands, guards, billing and the AN1/AN2 team aggregates only. No screen showed "von <Name>" from them.
3. **Corrected (`lca` 02:45; the first version of this ADR said no RLS4-2 column records an act, which was wrong):** `cover_events.nurse_id` records an act on `accepted` and `withdrawn` rows (with her free-text reason on a withdrawal); both `performer_id` columns allowed per-colleague lists of report times and return reasons. The PO moved all three to RLS4-1 (RLS4-5); built as such.
4. **Existing per-person paths, unchanged:** `member_open_reports(user)` (LEAVE1 part (b), accepted: kind, visit date, team of one leaver's open reports, for administrators and the team's registered nurses at access end) and the command `remove_team_member` take a person and read reports. pgTAP 099 pins that list, so no new one appears unnoticed. Whether the LEAVE1 reader stays as it is under D-23 is the PO's call (OPEN).
5. **"Teammitglied"** on `/visits` when the responsible member has no display name: the brief names only "<Name>" and "Noch offen" (wording as VIS2 deviation 1).
6. **Tests moved (OBS1-C1 pattern):** 016, 020, 022 assert the stored countersigner, author and performer as the table owner; 043, 049 mark the fixture helpers `head_fact`, `head_wt`, `report_v` `security definer` (they returned `select *` rows; asserted values unchanged); 057 checks the read on `responsible_absent`; 096's exact closed list gains the four `visit_reports` columns. Playwright F0 (`access.spec.ts`) tells the teams apart by service instead of the legacy names, which `/visits` no longer shows.
7. **Not in either list, unchanged:** `cases.proposed_primary_nurse_id` and `signal_takes.taker_id` were already not selectable; `quality_event_types.approved_by` is text. The *Not care records* line is unchanged.

**Not done (not coded):** no row read is widened or narrowed (no policy changed); no new per-person list, count or export; no clinical, legal or tariff rule; `team_member_name` not dropped; the live project is not touched (D-1).

**Checked by me (not CI):** migrations 0001-0099 and the seed on local PostgreSQL 16 with Supabase auth stubs and a minimal pgTAP stand-in; 0099 applies twice; every pgTAP file green with 0099 except 001 and 007 (stand-in gaps, identical without 0099); 099 63/63 with 0099 and 19 red without it; 071, 096 red without 0099 only on their RLS4 lines. `getVisitResponsibleLabel` checked in Node (type-stripped). vitest, lint, typecheck, build, real pgTAP, db lint, Playwright **not run** here (no npm registry); CI decides. The first version of #715 (22 columns) was green in CI on `6aaf09b`, `eec4f18`, `5f79caa`.

**Rollback:** revert the commit; `grant select on public.<table> to authenticated` for the 15 tables that had a table-wide grant, and `grant select (<column>)` for the closed columns on `visit_reports` (four), `case_gp_notices`, `cases`, `safety_signals`; restore `/visits` from `recovery/main`.

### ADR-123 — ASM1: the needs assessment is one append-only record; the care plan shows the assessment it rests on, the assessed minutes beside its items, never a sum

**Date:** 2026-10-30 · **Status:** IMPLEMENTED in its own PR (CI decides) · **Brief:** `docs/agents/briefs/ASM1.md` (approved 2026-10-27; amended 21:15 `aia` AUT-2, 22:15 `cra` CGR-81, 2026-10-28 02:10 `cra` CGR-84) · **Decision basis:** D-13 option B (po-decided 2026-10-25, criteria 1-5 from `lca` 01:41 and `cra` 02:26), D-36 option A (`lca` terms (a)-(c)) · **Clinical review gate:** applies; `cra` reads the merged build (CGR-81 and CGR-84 close on it) · **Migration:** 0100 · **Tests:** pgTAP 100 (74), 067 (+1 sweep line, two catalogue lists), vitest `asm1-assessment`, Playwright `asm1.spec.ts`.

**Context (IMPLEMENTED before 0100, read in a database built from 0001-0099):** the assessment is a visit flag (`care_visits.is_needs_assessment`) and a first-completion date (`cases.needs_assessment_done_at`, `coalesce`, 0014). `care_plan_items` carry service, category, minutes per occurrence and a free-text frequency (optional PLAN2 structure, 0025), linked to nothing. J5 billing facts take the visit's minutes (`care_visits.planned_minutes_*`, measured times; 0022, 0023, 0043), never the care plan's. No member stores a GLN (ADM1: name, role, team, qualification); qualification verification is D-29 (owner, open).

**Decision:**
1. **ASM1-6 `assessment_instruments`** per organisation: `name`, `version`, `valid_from`, `valid_to`, `third_party_mark` (the list shows "(Marke Dritter)"). No designation, logo, item text or licence claim (D-36 A, D-37). Every member of the organisation reads the list (no personal data); `created_by`, `ended_by` are not selectable. Administrators add an entry (`add_assessment_instrument`) and end one (`end_assessment_instrument`, refused with `records_after_end` when a record is dated after the new end). The seed holds three synthetic names. No admin screen yet (PLANNED; the seed and the commands serve the pilot list).
2. **ASM1-1 `assessment_records`**, append-only, several per case: `assessment_date`, `instrument_id`, `assessor_id`, `document_ref`, `minutes_a/b/c` with `period_a/b/c`, `supersedes_id`, `supersede_reason`, `entered_by`, `created_at` (and organisation, case, team). No item, score, scale, CAP, output or finding column (pgTAP pins the column list).
   - **Period (CGR-84):** the text entered, non-empty, at most 60 characters; no check on its values, no enum, no reference list, no default, nothing derived (pgTAP: no constraint on a period column contains a list or a pattern). Minutes without a period, or a period without minutes, are refused (constraint and command).
   - **Minutes:** integer 0..99999 per category or none. 99999 is a storage bound with no tariff or clinical meaning.
   - **Assessor:** a member of the case's assigned team with the recorded qualification `registered_nurse` whose access has not ended (the J3 rule `private.is_registered_nurse`). Verified qualification: **UNKNOWN until D-29**. **GLN: not stored** (no member field exists; who verifies it is D-29). The brief's "or her GLN where recorded" is therefore not built.
   - **Date:** not after today (Zurich). A data-integrity rule, not a clinical one.
3. **ASM1-3 reference bound:** `^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_:#-]{2,63}$` and at least one digit: 3..64 characters, no space, no dot, no slash. So no file name ("befund.pdf"), path ("akte/2026") or title without a digit ("Bedarfsabklaerung"). A title with a digit can still pass; the form says it is the identifier from the provider's tool. NAVORA stores no document.
4. **ASM1-4:** a record with any minutes needs the reference (constraint and command).
5. **ASM1-2 supersede:** a new record names the record it supersedes (same case, `unique (supersedes_id)`, so a superseded record is not superseded again: the correction is corrected instead) with a reason of 10..500 characters. Update, delete and truncate are refused for every role, the table owner included (`private.forbid_audit_change`). The superseded record stays, is marked "Ersetzt" in the history and is never current.
6. **ASM1-5 confirmation:** a record entered by the nurse it names is confirmed. Otherwise it is `unconfirmed` ("nicht bestätigt") until that nurse calls `confirm_assessment`, which writes one row of the append-only `assessment_confirmations` (audited: who and when). Nobody else can confirm (command and BEFORE INSERT trigger). A superseded record is not confirmed any more. Planning may use an unconfirmed record; the team sees the state.
7. **Current assessment:** the non-superseded record with the latest `assessment_date` (then the latest entry). Confirmation does not decide which is current.
8. **ASM1-7 care plan basis:** `care_plan_items.assessment_id`, set by a BEFORE INSERT trigger to the case's current record when the item is written (a value the writer passes is overwritten). Items written before 0100 keep null; no history is guessed. The plan's basis date is the oldest current version (supersede chain followed) among its active items' records; "Neuere Bedarfsabklärung vom <Datum>" is returned when the current record is dated later, or when the plan has an item written before any record. No block, signal, exception or task. Re-basing the plan without rewriting its items is **OPEN** (see deviation 2).
9. **ASM1-8:** no planning, readiness or billing function reads the records. `cases.needs_assessment_done_at` keeps its meaning (the first completed assessment visit) and no record writes it. pgTAP 100 compares the seed's billing preview, billable facts and visit minutes before and after records and a supersede: identical.
10. **ASM1-9 reads:** the records and confirmations are read by the members of the case's assigned team (RLS2 `private.reads_case`); `assessor_id`, `entered_by` and `confirmed_by` are not selectable through the table (RLS4 pattern) and are read per record only through `public.team_case_assessments()` (assessor's display name and `assessor_is_me`; who entered it is not returned). Operations, coaches and administrators read no record, minutes or reference (no policy, no reader; pgTAP for another organisation, Operations, coach, administrator). The reader carries the LEAVE1-3 guard. Nothing is widened.
11. **ASM1-11 (4) dated facts that exist today:** a resume after a pause and a pause for a hospital stay (`case_lifecycle_events`, C1 0024; the RET1 `review_after_pause` task follows the resume, so it is not listed twice), a serious quality event (`quality_events` of the case, severity high or critical as `private.is_serious` already defines it for QG1/OPS1). Each kind with its latest date when younger than the current assessment. **UNKNOWN / not built:** a hospital transfer as its own fact (only the pause reason `hospital_stay` exists), any other event. No new event type.
12. **ASM1-10/-11 screens** on the case card of `/team`: section "Bedarfsabklärung" (current record: date, instrument and version, assessor, state, minutes with period per category, reference; history with replaced records marked; record form; "Korrigieren (ersetzen)" with reason; "Als abklärende Pflegefachperson bestätigen" for the named nurse only). On the care plan: the basis date, the marker, and per category A, B, C the current record's minutes with their period beside the plan's items of that category as written (service, minutes per occurrence, frequency text, PLAN2 structure where recorded). No total, sum, average, difference, conversion, colour, icon or warning; the component has no conditional class (unit test renders two seeds with different minutes: identical classes).

**Trade-offs:** a correction needs every field again (the form is prefilled except instrument and assessor, which are chosen again so a stale entry is not carried over silently). The plan basis is per item, so a plan rewritten item by item moves its basis item by item. The reader returns every case of the caller's teams in one call (`/team` already lists them all).

**Deviations / notes for the PO:**
1. **GLN not built** (brief open question 2): no member field exists and D-29 names no verifier; the assessor is the team's registered nurse as recorded. Real-data gate unchanged (D-29, D-11/D-12).
2. **Re-basing the care plan (OPEN, po):** after a newer assessment the marker stays until the items are retired and written again. Whether a team may mark the plan as reviewed against the newer assessment, and who (a registered nurse?), is a product and possibly clinical question; nothing was invented.
3. **"Teilweise geschrieben gegen …"**: when some active items predate every record, the basis line says so in words (the brief names only the date).
4. **No instrument admin screen** in this PR (commands and pgTAP only; the pilot list is seeded synthetic). PLANNED with UX2 or a later admin brief.
5. **J5 (brief open question 1):** J5 does not read care-plan minutes today, so no migration path is needed; ASM1 makes nothing the billing source.

**Not done (not coded):** no reassessment interval, due date, reminder, signal or exception; no category derived; no import (D-13 A); no designated instrument (D-37); no sum or comparison; no GLN; the live project is not touched (D-1).

**Checked by me (not CI):** migrations 0001-0100 and the seed on local PostgreSQL 16 with Supabase auth stubs and a minimal pgTAP stand-in; 0100 applies twice; pgTAP 100 74/74; every other pgTAP file unchanged against the 0099 baseline once 067 (two catalogue lists, one sweep line) is updated and 0100 follows ADR-081 (no native `now()` or Zurich conversion) and LEAVE1-3 (reader guard), which 068 and 074 caught. **Not run:** real pgTAP, vitest, typecheck, lint, build, Playwright (no npm registry access in this run): CI decides.

**Rollback:** revert the commit; `drop table public.assessment_confirmations, public.assessment_records, public.assessment_instruments cascade`, `alter table public.care_plan_items drop column assessment_id`, re-create `audit_care_plan_items` with its four columns, drop the four commands, the reader and the `private` helpers.

### ADR-124 — UX2-22 / UX2-23 ahead of the shell: the screen offers only what the server allows her, and a switch with organisation-wide consequences asks once

**Date:** 2026-10-30 · **Status:** IMPLEMENTED in its own PR (CI decides) · **Brief:** `docs/agents/briefs/UX2.md` UX2-22 (`uxa` UXR-45; `cra` 13:40 PR #666: hide; `uxa` 16:10 one line) and UX2-23 (`uxa` UXR-43; `uxa` 16:10 wording), both G-23 preconditions, delivered ahead of the rest of UX2 as the brief allows (po 2026-10-29 12:15) · **Clinical review gate:** applies (`/field`); `cra` already decided the presentation (hide); `cra` reads the build in its UX2 gate · **Migration:** none · **Tests:** vitest `ux2-gates`, Playwright `ux2-gates.spec` (390 px), pgTAP 002 and 018 adjusted for the fixture.

**Context (IMPLEMENTED before this PR):** ADM2-16 (0094) refuses depart, start, resume and complete for a caller without a recorded qualification in the visit's team (`qualification_missing`, one audit row); `team_open_visits.may_take` is already false for her. `/field` did not read her qualification, so it offered "Losfahren", "Einsatz starten" and the completion control on her team's visits and listed "Offen im Team" with its toggle, each tap ending in the refusal. On `/admin` "Einschalten"/"Ausschalten" (ADM2-1) and "Vertragsende entfernen" (ADM2-11) ran their command on the first tap.

**Decision:**
1. **One read, no new scope (UX2-22):** `/field` reads her own `team_memberships` rows (`team_id, qualification`, filter `user_id = auth.uid()`; RLS `read_own_team_membership`, 0003) and derives the teams where hers is null (`teamsWithoutQualification`, pure). Nothing about another member is read.
2. **Hide, per team (`cra` 13:40):** a visit card of such a team offers none of depart, start, resume, complete (`actionsForMember`). Pause is not refused by 0094 and stays (in practice she cannot have started a visit after 0094). No-show, "nicht abgeschlossen", cancel, hold, "Heute machen", observations and the report section are unchanged (0094 and ADR-117 deviation 1 do not gate them). A visit of a team that is not hers (cover) is left to the server, which checks the cover team.
3. **The sentence once:** `QUALIFICATION_MISSING_MESSAGE` (unchanged) in `info`, directly under the SIG1 signals (they stay above all content, UX2-7) and above everything else.
4. **"Offen im Team":** open visits of such a team are withheld. When none of the open visits is of a team where she is qualified, the section keeps its heading without a count, `info` (not the orange frame), one line "Offene Einsätze übernehmen Teammitglieder mit erfasster Qualifikation."; no client name, time, toggle or "Übernehmen". With nothing open at all the section is absent, as for everyone.
5. **One confirming step (UX2-23):** a small `ConfirmStep` opens in place of the control: focus on its sentence, the action (`action-primary`) and "Abbrechen" (`action-outline`), 44 px. "Abbrechen" runs nothing (no command, no audit row); the action runs the existing command once. Sentences in `src/features/admin/confirm-texts.ts`, pinned by vitest.
6. **Fixture:** Testorganisation D in `supabase/seed.sql` (its own organisation): `ohnequal.test` (signs in; the one seeded member without a qualification), `admind.test` (administrator; team admission on), `vertragd.test` (contract end in 30 days, never signs in), one planned visit today 21:00 with nobody responsible. pgTAP 002 counts 7 → 9; pgTAP 018's "no member without a qualification" excludes exactly this fixture and a new assertion pins it (plan 25 → 26). Checked on a local PostgreSQL 16 build (migrations 0001-0100 and the seed apply; as `ohnequal` the own-row read returns one row with null, `field_visits` and `team_open_visits` return the visit, `start_visit` returns `qualification_missing`; as `admind` `set_member_contract_end` and `set_team_admission` return `ok`). pgTAP itself not run locally.

**Deviations / notes for the PO:**
1. **UX2-23 second off-sentence dropped** (the brief's own fallback). As built, switching team admission off does **not** leave proposals resting: the team's and the administrators' readers (`team_admissions`, `admin_team_admissions`, 0091) run `private.adm2_sweep` whatever the switch, and the sweep (as patched in 0092) lapses an open proposal 14 Zurich days after it was made and an admitted one without a recorded contract 14 days after its admission (ADM2-8). "ruhen, bis wieder eingeschaltet wird" would be false, so only "Danach nimmt nur die Administration neue Mitglieder auf." is shown. Whether lapsing should pause while off is a product question (po), not built.
2. **Multi-team member:** the brief speaks of "her team". Built per team: controls hidden only on visits of teams where hers is missing; the sentence shows when any of her teams lacks it; "Offen im Team" shows the one line only when every open visit is of such a team, otherwise the list of the others as built.
3. **Cover offers** ("Abdeckung"): 0091 already refuses a cover accept without a qualification (`cover_team_for`); the brief does not name the cover section, so it is unchanged here. Noted for the shell work (same rule as UX2-22: offer only what she may do).
4. **e2e for a qualified member** is not duplicated: field.spec, ux1.spec and vis1.spec still assert the controls for qualified nurses and are unchanged.
5. **team-admission.spec** changed by one selector step per switch use (the step's own button), as UX2-7 allows.

**Not done (not coded):** the rest of UX2 (shell, tokens, lint rule, first-MVP pages, UX2-26 qualification on the contract card); no migration; no rule, refusal code, read scope or `cra`/`lca` text changed; the live project is not touched (D-1).

**Checked by me (not CI):** migrations and seed on local PostgreSQL 16 with Supabase auth stubs (results above). **Not run:** pgTAP, vitest, typecheck, lint, build, Playwright (`npm ci` fails in this environment: "Exit handler never called"). CI is the test runner.

**Rollback:** revert the commit; the Testorganisation D block of the seed and the pgTAP 002/018 edits go with it.

### ADR-125 — ASM1-C1 to ASM1-C5: her confirmation work reaches her, the history folds, refusals state the rule, one review act re-bases the care plan without touching an item, and a corrected basis is visible

**Date:** 2026-10-30 · **Status:** IMPLEMENTED in its own PR (CI decides) · **Brief:** `docs/agents/briefs/ASM1.md`, PO note 08:15 (ASM1-C1 to -C3 from `uxa` UXR-54, -55, -59), PO decision 09:15 (ASM1-C4 from `aia` AUT-26), PO review 10:15 (`changes-requested`; ASM1-C5 from `cra` CGR-102; `cra` 09:30 conditions taken in) · **Clinical review gate:** applies; findings handled: CGR-102 (remediation plan `clinical/remediation/CGR-102.md`), `cra` 09:30 ASM1-C1/-C2/-C4 conditions; CGR-103 = ASM1-C6 not in this PR (deadline: before the first real assessment record; does not hold acceptance).

**Context (IMPLEMENTED, 0100 / #727):** `care_plan_items.assessment_id` is set on insert only; the plan's basis is the oldest head-of-chain date among active items. The marker clears only when every item is retired and retyped (ADR-123 deviation 2). A same-date correction of the basis record leaves no marker (CGR-102). The confirmation step (ASM1-5) is visible only inside the case card on `/team`. Every record shows its minutes with "Kategorie A/B/C" and the care-plan block shows them again.

**Decision:**
1. **ASM1-C4 review act, no item change.** New append-only `care_plan_assessment_reviews` (case, record named, the record's state at the act (`cra` (a)), the active item ids it covered, who, when; `seq` identity for order). The act **changes no `care_plan_items` row**: an update would fire PLAN2's `care_plan_items_regenerate` (0025) and the ORD0 triggers (0061, 0063), and `cra` (c) requires generation, visit minutes and J5 facts unchanged. An item's basis is `private.plan_item_basis(item, stored)`: the record of the latest act whose `item_ids` contain the item, else the record stored on its insert. Items written after an act get the current record on insert, so the latest of the two is always the right one; order is by `seq`, not by clock (pgTAP runs in one transaction with one `now()`).
2. **Command** `public.review_care_plan_against_assessment(case, record, key)`: a member of the case's assigned team (access not ended) **with the recorded qualification `registered_nurse`** (PO 09:15 provisional set, the J3 rule `private.is_registered_nurse`); the record must be the case's current non-superseded record; the plan must have an active item; and something must be shown (an item without basis, a basis whose head differs (ASM1-C5) or whose head date is older than the current record (ASM1-7)). Reasons: `not_team_member`, `not_rn`, `not_current`, `no_items`, `nothing_to_review`. Idempotent (`begin_command`); audited (`write_audit_event('case_id','record_id','record_state')`, AUD1 catalogue `code`).
3. **Reads (ASM1-C4 (4), RLS4 pattern):** the table has RLS on and **no grant and no policy**: nobody selects it. `public.team_case_assessments()` returns the case's latest act as `plan_review` (record, its date, state at the act, Zurich date, reviewer's display name), per case only, members of the case's team only. No count or list per person. Because no `authenticated` select exists, the RLS2-1 catalogue (pgTAP 067) is unchanged.
4. **ASM1-C5 (CGR-102):** the reader returns `corrected_basis_date` (the basis record's assessment date) and `corrected_on` (the Zurich date the head was entered) when an active item's basis differs from its head; the oldest such basis per case. The care-plan block shows "Die Bedarfsabklärung vom <Datum>, gegen die der Pflegeplan geschrieben ist, wurde am <Datum> korrigiert." at `info`. The act clears it.
5. **`team_case_assessments` re-created** (return type changes: `drop function` then `create`, grants repeated). Everything it returned before is returned unchanged, except that the basis now uses (1).
6. **ASM1-C1 (UXR-59):** `/field` "Zu erledigen" row shows "Zu bestätigen: Bedarfsabklärung (<n>)" when n ≥ 1, linking to `/team#bedarfsabklaerung-<first record id>`. n = records in her own reader output with `assessor_is_me`, `unconfirmed`, not superseded (`recordsToConfirm`, pure, unit-tested). Neutral classes (stone, `data-tone="info"`); no reminder, push or due date; nothing for anyone else. Disappears on confirm or supersede (both revalidate `/field`). Void (ASM1-C6) is not built yet; the filter gets it when C6 lands.
7. **ASM1-C2 (UXR-55):** the current record (line, state, actions, "Ersetzt eine frühere Erfassung. Grund: …") stays open; all other records are in a closed `details` "Frühere Erfassungen (<n>)" with their minutes in short form ("A: … · B: … · C: …", no "Kategorie" label). "Kategorie A/B/C" with the assessed minutes appears once per card, in the care-plan block. When the C1 link targets a record inside the history, the `details` opens.
8. **ASM1-C3 (UXR-54):** `date_in_future` → "Das Datum der Bedarfsabklärung darf nicht in der Zukunft liegen."; `assessor_not_rn` → "Als abklärende Person kannst du nur eine Pflegefachperson des Teams wählen."; the reference rule (`REFERENCE_HINT`) is shown under the field on the empty form, outside the label, tied by `aria-describedby`.
9. **UX2-30 carried in part:** the record form is one column at phone width (UXR-56); the replace form names the record it replaces (UXR-57, the "names" half; instrument and assessor are still chosen again, ADR-123 trade-off). UXR-58 (one error component) not done.

**Trade-offs:** an item covered by an act keeps its stored `assessment_id` (the record it was typed against); the effective basis lives in the act. Two places to read, but `care_plan_items` stays untouched and the act is the audit trail of the review. `item_ids` is an array, not a child table: one act, one row, append-only; `any()` is fine at a team's plan size.

**Deviations / notes for the PO:**
1. **ASM1-C6 not in this PR** (deadline before real data; the PO allowed Engineering to choose). OPEN; the C1 filter and the C2 history have the place for it.
2. **UXR-57 half / UXR-58** (UX2-30) not done; low, with UX2.
3. **The act is refused when nothing is shown** (`nothing_to_review`), so a team cannot record a review that changes nothing on screen. The brief says "available while the marker shows"; the refusal enforces it on the server too.

**Not done (not coded):** no due date, interval, reminder, signal or colour above `info`; no item changed; no void (C6); no per-person count; the live project is not touched (D-1).

**Checked by me (not CI):** migrations 0001-0101 and the seed on local PostgreSQL 16 with Supabase auth stubs and a minimal pgTAP stand-in (`plan`, `ok`, `is`, `finish`); 0101 applies twice; pgTAP 101 33/33 and 100 74/74 under the stand-in; every pgTAP file run on a 0001-0100 baseline and on 0001-0101: the only difference is the new file 101 (the stand-in's own failures, missing `throws_ok` etc. and privilege artefacts, are identical on both sides). vitest, typecheck, lint, real pgTAP and Playwright **not run here** (npm registry unreachable); CI decides.

**Addendum 2026-10-30 12:45 (ASM1-C4 (d), `uxa` UXR-60 #748, PO 12:14; same PR #747; presentation only, no migration):** (1) the review command and its hint are rendered **last** in the care-plan block, after the assessment line, the category rows and the newer-event lines; the marker and the ASM1-C5 sentence stay first. (2) Label "Als geprüft bestätigen: Pflegeplan gegen Bedarfsabklärung vom <Datum>" (`reviewLabel`); the recorded line keeps the participle (`planReviewText`, unchanged). (3) One confirming step with the UX2-23 `ConfirmStep` (reused from `admin/confirm-step.tsx`): the sentence names the record date and says that «Neuere Bedarfsabklärung» and «… korrigiert» disappear for the team and the items stay; "Abbrechen" runs nothing (no command, no act, no audit row). `cra` may drop (3). Tests: vitest `asm1-conditions` (DOM order, label, confirm step); Playwright `asm1.spec` (command below the last category row, cancel keeps the marker and writes no act line, confirm clears the marker).

**Rollback:** revert the commit; `drop function public.review_care_plan_against_assessment(uuid, uuid, uuid), private.plan_item_bases(uuid), private.plan_item_basis(uuid, uuid)`; `drop table public.care_plan_assessment_reviews`; re-create `public.team_case_assessments()` from 0100; delete the three `audit_value_classes` rows.
